# Roblox ad and promotion playbook

_Generated 2026-11-01 08:01 by `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/build_tools.py` from the topic files in this KB. Do not hand-edit; rebuild instead._

Everything the KB knows about paid ads (Roblox Ads Manager / sponsored experiences) and off-platform promotion, organised as a campaign plan: readiness gate, budget and schedule, targeting, creatives, metrics and stop rules, off-platform. **Roblox ad tools change; official docs (flag official) override older indie advice. Ads are retrieval/seed data, not a ranking signal, per official sources.**

Flag: **official** = Roblox Creator Docs / Roblox or Roblox Creators sessions; **mixed** = official and indie agree; **devforum** = DevForum post (staff or community, check the link); **indie** = creator self-reported, unverified. Official 2026 docs override older indie advice. Date = newest citation.


Counts: 412 rules, 298 numbers, 187 if-then rules, 70 disagreements.

> **Supplement 2026-11-01 (maintained in `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/supplements/`, re-inserted on every rebuild; full detail and trust labels in [[ads-stats-monetisation-playbook]]):**
> - Ads Manager auto-bids (you set budget + duration). Official average cost per play (DevForum staff 2025-08-05): Maximize Plays $0.008, Drive Retention $0.007, Reactivate $0.011 (upgrades the indie-only $0.008). OFFICIAL.
> - Current objective names (Creator Docs ads-manager, fetched 2026-11-01): Plays, Earnings (Limited), Engagement; audiences All / New (never or 180+ days) / Recent (30 days, 10,000+ players) / Lapsed (30-180 days, 20,000+). The 2025-08 names Acquire New Users / Drive Retention / Reactivate are superseded. OFFICIAL.
> - Learning period: docs say first 24 h, DevForum staff 2026-10-02 says 24-48 h and restarts on pause; plan for 48 h. OFFICIAL.
> - DevEx: 0.0038 USD/Robux (0.0054 qualifying 18+ US; 0.0035 before 2025-09-05); indie "0.038" is 10x wrong. Engagement-Based Payouts deprecated 2025-07-24 (Creator Rewards). OFFICIAL.
> - Earnings objective beta games had ARPU >= 6 Robux (H1 2026). OFFICIAL (DevForum 2026-10-02).
> - Retention anchor (third party, secondary, SINGLE-SOURCE): GameAnalytics 2026 report, 500+ Roblox games over 1M MAU, Aug 2025-Jul 2026: D1 median 10.3% (top 1% 22.2%), D7 1.6% (9.1%), D30 0.5% (4.7%); session median 9.8 min; payer conversion 3.80%. Excludes games under 1M MAU.
> - Rewarded video: Roblox publishes NO EPM value (docs 2026-11-01); eligibility 2,000 monthly uniques (docs) replaced the 100K DAU launch rule (2025-07). Influencer pricing: no published price list; anecdotal only.

## Default campaign recipe (from master playbook; verify against rules below)

1. Gate: only advertise when D1, first-play funnel and PTR are decent (fix retention first; ads cannot rescue a weak game).
2. Seed test: about $5-20/day for 7-11 days, broad targeting, 2-4 creatives, one change at a time.
3. Read cohort retention, cost per play/engaged player and ROAS; keep what beats your break-even.
4. Stop or shrink when organic Home recommendations exceed sponsored impressions.
5. Refresh creatives at major updates.

(Recipe wording comes from the master playbook decision rules; the exact figures are reported in sections below with flags.)


## 1. Readiness gate: when NOT to run ads (97)

- **Scale gradually when ROI is proven** [official, 2026-10-12, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Start small (2018 official: ~500 Robux; reinvest returns). Scale only if retention of ad users holds. InGame went from $250/day to double and triple spend while ROAS stayed above 100%. CallMeDogey reinvested earned Robux into ads. -- [Roblox Creators 2025-10 27:20](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1640) [Roblox 2025-09 6:49](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=409)
- **Use Ads Manager objectives and audiences as a funnel** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Maximize Plays for launches, mid-life games or updates; Drive Retention for players of the last 0-30 days; Reactivate for lapsed users; Earnings for ROAS (needs a developed economy). Example split 60/20/20. Major update on an old game: Plays + Reactivate for 1-2 weeks. Audiences: New = no play 180 days; Recent = last 30 days (10,000 mini… -- [Roblox Creators 2025-10 16:24](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=984) [Roblox Creators 2025-06 28:42](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1722)
- **Ads don't hurt organic ranking and are excluded from RFY signals** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Official: ad-acquired users' engagement, retention and spending are excluded from RFY ranking signals, so ads add incremental players. Roblox cites impression lift in 99% of 370 advertised experiences. Some RFY impressions may dip as users move to the Continue sort. Indies counter that ads don't push you up on their own. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- **Measure ads by quality, not clicks** [official, 2026-10-28, analytics-metrics]: Use Ads Manager as source of truth (Creator Analytics blends sources). Wait ~24h learning and 48h reporting delay; check ROI at day 30. Track CTR, CPP, play rate, 7D playtime and 30D Robux per converted player. MoseyTube targets ~2.5% CTR and ~$0.008 per play (self-reported); from day 5 stop ads and fix gameplay if retention is poor. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing) [Roblox Creators 2025-06 50:08](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3008)
- **Gate beta exits on age mix, funnel and like ratio** [official, 2026-10-28, analytics-metrics]: During a beta, track tutorial completion, drop-off points, D1/D7 retention, session length and age demographics. Check that the actual age mix matches the intended target, since a mismatch signals a design or marketing problem. Aim for at least an 80% like ratio after closing. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences)
- **Keep experiences and events private until ready** [official, 2026-10-28, development-process-and-speed]: A game saved while private stays undiscoverable until you publish publicly. Create events as private first, and switch to public only when promoting them. Private events don't show on the experience detail page. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events)
- **Only organic Recommended For You plays count for ranking** [official, 2026-10-28, discovery-algorithm]: Ranking signals use only players who clicked from the RFY sort on Home. Ads, search, friend and direct-link plays are excluded from ranking signals. Later sessions after an RFY first play still count toward the 7-day signals. Ads and editorial features can feed retrieval but do not train ranking. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq) [Roblox Creators 2026-06 26:16](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=1576)
- **Treat Twitter as community-building and skip early paid influencers** [official, 2025-09-26, social-features-and-virality]: Twitter engagement does not easily convert to Roblox sales; Ninja Challenge relied on organic creator interest rather than paid marketing. -- [Roblox 2025-09 32:10](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1930) [Roblox Creators 2025-08 14:47](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=887)
- **Run up to 10 ad creatives per campaign and disable underperformers** [official, 2026-10-28, thumbnails-icons]: Upload up to 10 creatives per campaign and compare per-creative performance in campaign details. Hypershot went from 10 to 25 unique creatives to find high-CTR visuals, then paused weaker ones. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise)
- **Promote events both externally and inside the game** [official, 2026-10-28, updates-live-ops]: Use social media and community tools for reach, and internal surfaces such as login pop-ups, UI alerts and lobby prominence for players already in the experience. One channel alone under-delivers. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning)
- **Fix retention before buying ads** [mixed, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Ads are the fastest way to get players but won't fix a bad game. Paid traffic is wasted on weak retention. An indie test with a weak game peaked at ~20 CCU then fell to near zero. Only advertise games whose organic retention and playtime are already good. Ads cannot reverse declining organic engagement. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition) [kra_DeZ 2026-10 8:21](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=501)
- **Treat brand integrations as in-game quests, not standalone games** [mixed, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Brands pay for integrations inside popular games; standalone brand games usually get almost no players (over 99% per Row Monitor). Branded IP lowers UA cost but retention still needs fun. Choose brand partners that fit your vibe, weave content into the core loop; limited activations run 2-4 weeks. - **Prioritise retention (D1/D7/D30) as the core health metric** [mixed, 2026-10-28, analytics-metrics]: Retention is the key metric; without it you tread water with constant updates. Track D1, D7, D30 and week-later returns to judge updates. Use D1 plus average session time as early indicators of long-term retention. Check D1/D7 against benchmarks before paying to promote a new game. -- [Roblox Creators 2021-07 35:47](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2147) [Roblox Creators 2021-07 1:00:29](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3629)
- **Use Home Recommendation signals for discovery diagnosis** [mixed, 2026-10-28, analytics-metrics]: Check Acquisition > Home Recommendations: play-through rate, play days and playtime first, then secondary signals (spend days, co-play, Deep play-through, 7-day qualified sessions, bounce rate where lower is better). Fix the weakest signal. These diagnose organic distribution, not ads. - **Don't judge or abandon a quiet game before promoting it and checking quality** [mixed, 2026-09-14, common-mistakes]: Most failures come from nobody knowing it exists; good games can flop at launch and blow up later; items can have zero sales then trend weeks later. - **Do not rely on ads or Robux spend to rescue a weak game** [indie+devforum, 2026-05-16, common-mistakes]: Ad spikes without retention burn budget and players fall back to zero. - **Make the ad promise match what the game delivers** [indie, 2026-05-10, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Lnor saw higher playtime but worse day-1 retention from ad traffic, which pointed to an audience or messaging mismatch. Make thumbnails and copy describe what the first minutes actually deliver, then judge by D1 retention. -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122)
- **Reuse the thumbnail that already won** [indie, 2026-09-12, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: LovJak reuses the thumbnail that previously performed best (a banana thumbnail) in new ad campaigns. Lnor saw a slight CTR gain from AI-redone thumbnails. Establish a proven baseline before testing variants. -- [LovJak 2026-09 2:46](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=166) [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 1:22](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=82)
- **Test 18+ (O18) audiences in paid acquisition where policy allows** [indie, 2026-09-30, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Roblox Investor Relations (Sep 2026) reports paid UA targeting O18 users retains, engages and monetizes better than organic signups. Test an O18 segment against an all-player baseline on day-7 retention and Robux per user. Keep the ROI bar high while scaling. Confirm age rating and audience rules first. -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- **Mine past campaign data for patterns, then validate in a controlled test** [indie, 2021-11-10, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Use legacy campaign data to find placement and duration sweet spots, and use them to rule out weak placements and prioritise creative production. Confirm any pattern in a controlled test before relying on it for budget decisions. -- [GameMakers 2021-11 26:44](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=1604) [GameMakers 2021-11 39:05](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=2345) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Measure CPI against forecast LTV early, and re-check as CPI rises** [indie, 2025-02-04, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Spend a small UA budget early to measure the CPI vs LTV gap, and track retention at the same time. CPI rises over time from audience saturation, new competing apps and rivals improving, so standing still erodes profitability. -- [GameMakers 2025-02 12:12](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=732) [GameMakers 2022-08 38:20](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2300) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Use churn per 1,000 impressions as an ad-quality check** [indie, 2026-02-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Losing about 1 user per 1,000 impressions is fine; 100 per 1,000 is very bad. Ad networks optimise for CPM, not your ARPDAU, and the publisher pays the retention cost, so do not raise monetisation by letting retention fall. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 8:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=509) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 7:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=449)
- **Test a new country with a small paid campaign before any influencer push** [indie, 2026-06-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Run a small paid UA campaign in the new country first. Scale to influencers only if retention, CPI or ROI look good, then move the market to always-on. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 30:49](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1849)
- **Treat influencer marketing as a permanent pillar with set expectations** [indie, 2026-06-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: First campaigns are for learning, and influencer marketing needs budget and time before it becomes profitable. Cutting spend increased install decline in one 10-year-old game. Giveaway campaigns show a lower organic share than non-giveaway ones. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 43:13](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2593) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 4:47](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=287)
- **Judge a game by income per CCU, not concurrency alone** [indie, 2026-07-05, analytics-metrics]: A 100-CCU game with poor income is a weak purchase. When evaluating a game or a monetisation change, compare income per concurrent user against spend. janisjanis's Shorts-promoted game spent 369,000 Robux and earned 64,506 (net -304,494). -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 2:45](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=165) [janisjanis01 2026-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730)
- **Stop ads once organic Home Recommendations appear** [indie, 2026-08-26, analytics-metrics]: Check Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition for a Home Recommendations bar. Once it appears, turn ads off and let organic recommendations run. After first recs, ship one targeted update (a retention fix or better early tutorial) and check whether recs grow. -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 14:09](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=849) [VuukStudios 2026-08 8:52](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=532)
- **Read ad dashboards as 7-day moving averages over complete windows** [indie, 2026-08-07, analytics-metrics]: Day-one snapshots of paid traffic were poor and improved by day two. Use 7-day moving averages and wait for full windows: day-7 retention is meaningless before a week has passed and shows 'not yet available' until then. Paid players are cold, so engagement and day-1 retention dip during ads and should be judged over a longer window. -- [LovJak 2026-08 6:49](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=409) [LovJak 2026-07 4:48](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=288)
- **Measure organic uplift with a per-market proxy model** [indie, 2026-06-10, analytics-metrics]: Track organic installs in each market before, during and after a campaign, not just tracked installs. Attribution alone understates influencer impact and can hide a path to profitability. The lift is often much larger than attributed installs. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:05](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=905) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 8:13](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=493)
- **Expect about 30% organic uplift and vary it by campaign type** [indie, 2026-06-10, analytics-metrics]: Typical organic uplift in test markets was about 30% for Match Masters. Giveaway campaigns attribute traffic directly, so uplift is lower (~30%). Non-giveaway posts can have up to ~100% of attributed traffic be organic. Expect variation by game, audience and country. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=659) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 9:36](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=576)
- **Compare verticals on CPM, view-to-install, LTV and retention, with minimum sample** [indie, 2026-06-01, analytics-metrics]: Diagnose which metric is failing before changing creators. Do not trust a vertical comparison below 10+ videos and about 500k combined views. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343)
- **Keep an owned raw data copy and reconcile with MMP and store dashboards** [indie, 2025-02-04, analytics-metrics]: Set up analytics and a data lake you own before spending on user acquisition. Do not rely only on MMP or store dashboards; verify data consistency first. -- [GameMakers 2025-02 10:26](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=626)
- **Stabilise and check ad performance before scaling spend** [indie, 2026-05-31, common-mistakes]: Bugs wasted a 32,000 Robux ad spend and killed retention on earlier games. Check click-through early: about 1.9% CTR means replace the creative, not add spend. Scale only after a stable build. -- [Duckable 2026-05 10:09](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=609) [janisjanis01 2026-05 9:19](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=559)
- **Fix quality and economy bugs before paid promotion or sponsorship** [indie, 2026-07-09, common-mistakes]: Sponsoring or advertising an unpolished game wastes spend. Poke saw player count drop to zero once the ad ended. ThePurpleGuy's beta bugs cost six dislikes and forced a pause and relaunch. LovJak found a selling price equal to purchase price, which leaves no profit. Check retention and economy first. -- [LovJak 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=0) [ThePurpleGuy 2026-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/VEkuM5ZG6G4?t=0)
- **Diagnose execution and friction before scrapping the concept** [indie, 2026-08-18, common-mistakes]: A dead game may be an execution problem (polish, friction) rather than a bad idea. - **Set expected KPIs by market and device before soft launch** [indie, 2024-10-24, common-mistakes]: Before soft launch, write down expected LTV, retention and crash rate per market and device. - **Choose promotion venues carefully** [indie, 2026-02-20, community-and-discord]: Promote in Discord promo channels only with permission. - **Use mobile ads to drive cross-platform installs** [indie, 2026-10-26, cross-platform-and-console]: One indie developer (self-reported) used mobile ads to bring players into a cross-platform experience and reports that most of their users play on both mobile and PC. - **Stop ads once Home recommendations sustain the game** [indie, 2026-09-27, discovery-algorithm]: Stop paid ads when organic Home traffic sustains the game, for example when the lowest-CCU time still fills two lobbies. - **Rotate environment states and spawn events on short, documented timers** [indie, 2026-09-19, events-and-seasonal-content]: Angel and demon biomes rotate about every 5 minutes (streamer estimate), and a rift event spawns about every 30 minutes. - **Measure attributed and organic uplift** [indie, 2026-06-10, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: Use a proxy uplift model comparing organic installs before, during and after the campaign in that market. - **Run as a performance channel alongside paid UA** [indie, 2026-06-10, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: Track installs in internal analytics and compare retention against other sources. - **Take responsibility for results and diagnose** [indie, 2026-09-14, mindset-and-strategy]: When metrics are bad, ask what you did wrong or can improve in yourself rather than blaming players, algorithm, devs or saturation. - **Confirm product-market fit before moving to live ops** [indie, 2022-08-30, mindset-and-strategy]: Treat new-game development as a search for product-market fit: healthy retention plus high-converting ad creatives at a competitive CPI. - **Scale paid spend only after an organic baseline exists** [indie, 2024-10-24, mindset-and-strategy]: GameMakers (2024-10) says larger scale can lift organic coverage and k-factor and can unlock better ad-network terms, but the bet is risky if the strategy is wrong. - **Budget paid user acquisition for rising competition** [indie, 2026-09-30, monetisation-other]: Roblox reports over 200 paid UA campaigns this summer and O18 marketing spend up nearly 3x YoY. - **Verify the game loads and is playable with real testers before paid ads** [indie, 2026-05-10, playtesting-and-feedback]: Low play rate was blamed on loading issues in one case. - **Read ad trends over 90-180 days, not one snapshot** [indie, 2022-03-01, trend-research-and-validation]: Track a genre's ad intelligence over 90-180 days to see when a trend started, peaked and faded. A single snapshot cannot show direction, so a genre that looks hot today may already be fading. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 21:12](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1272) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Ship fresh content and a polished page before buying ads** [indie, 2026-08-07, updates-live-ops]: Launch new content (fruits, levels, events) and set the icon and thumbnail before running paid campaigns, so paid traffic lands in a fresh game. Plan the next update and its event calendar in advance. -- [LovJak 2026-08 4:45](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=285) [LovJak 2026-07 1:20](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=80)

## 2. Budget, duration and schedule (135)

- **Iterate onboarding in small steps** [official, 2026-10-30, ab-testing-and-experiments]: Roblox-featured example: each change retained a bit more while spending on ads, and the algorithm picked the game up around day 7-8. Self-reported. -- [Roblox Creators 2026-10 13:45](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=825) [Roblox Creators 2026-10 15:49](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=949)
- **Run campaigns at least 1-2 weeks, not 1-2 days** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Official Roblox guidance (2025-06): short 1-2 day campaigns don't give the system time to optimise. Official docs say allow 3-5 days for results before judging. Older indie advice of 1-day or 3-day bursts is superseded. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager) [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- **Try search ads and bid on relevant keywords** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Search ads (45M searches/day) often bring users with better engagement and monetisation than sponsored experience users. Check Audience Estimator volume, favour high-volume keywords with few bidders, bid only on relevant keywords, max 10 per Ad Set, single-keyword Ad Sets to compare. Search ads use the Visits objective, 13+, no audience… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads) [Roblox 2024-09 6:43](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=403)
- **Get campaign payment and billing rules right** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Ads Manager needs 13+, an ad account and a payment method (card or Robux converted to ad credits). Card payments charge at $100 or month end, daily cap $300, $5 first-time charge, payment method locked after publish. Ad credits: unused credit refunded as ad credit in 24-48h (never Robux). Fraud refunds at 16 days. Cancel up to 6 hours be… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing) [Roblox Creators 2026-02 0:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=4)
- **Register and moderate ad assets early** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: For in-experience ads, submit campaigns at least 5 days before start; assets are moderated typically within 48 hours. Put new assets in a separate fixed-date campaign, since during review the whole campaign serves only 18+. Check AdService:GetCampaignEligibilityAsync. Self-promotional assets with calls to action also count as ads. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations)
- **Place rewarded video where it feels like earning** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Use multiple placements in high-traffic spots (HUD, lobby, shop, loss screen), clear reward text, immediate reward, no forced ads or randomised rewards, no Robux rewards, a daily cap, boosts lasting one match/session. - **Measure rewarded video with the API and key metrics** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Track opt-in rate and daily frequency. Call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync right before showing, not earlier, and not after an ad unless offering another. Use placement IDs and RegisterAdOpportunityAsync. - **Promote with hype and launch events** [official, 2026-10-12, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Official: teaser trailers on YouTube/Twitter ending with date/time; daily short teasers across nine social accounts with a partner UGC item event; speculation content as cheap prerelease hype; portals in-experience (up to 27% of monetisation for some publishers). -- [Roblox Creators 2024-08 19:32](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1172) [Roblox 2023-09 14:12](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=852)
- **Verify campaign eligibility before creating a campaign** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Call campaign-options for the universe first. A NO_PERMISSION or BLOCKED response means the universe cannot advertise. Confirm this before writing any campaign-creation code or spending. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Send an idempotency key on campaign creation** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Include a UUID in x-idempotency-key. Replaying the same key with an identical body within 24 hours returns the original campaign, which prevents duplicate campaigns and double spend. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Set campaign length with durationInDays, not endTime** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: endTime returns a 400 in v1. durationInDays has a maximum of 3650 days. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Raise budgets carefully and expect decreases to wait for midnight** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Budget increases apply immediately. Decreases apply at the next midnight in the billing account time zone. Raise budgets in small steps to avoid surprise spend. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Use PAUSED to stop spend temporarily; CANCELLED is permanent** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: CANCELLED cannot be undone. Use PAUSED to halt spend and ACTIVE to resume a paused campaign. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Set audience, name and daily budget when creating a sponsored item** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Creating a sponsored item requires a target audience (gender, age, platform) and a descriptive name, which only you can see. Minimum bid is 10 Robux per day, and ad cycles run 24 hours to 28 days. Display share scales with your daily budget relative to rival bidders. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items)
- **Run a limited-time test with about $100-250 of ad space** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Roblox Creator Docs (Oct 2026) suggests spending roughly $100-250 USD on Roblox ad space to drive traffic to a limited-time test window. Revenue share is typically 15-30% and covers purchases, ads and creator rewards. Read content standards (no mixing third-party IP, no actor likenesses). Fix flagged issues within 10 business days. - **Check performance, audience and pillars continuously; soft-launch** [official, 2026-09-30, development-process-and-speed]: Check performance during development, not at the end. Limited-time game pass free trials gave 4%+ more Robux spend and spenders. Rewarded-ad trials of high-value passes were used in Brookhaven. Older paid perks can later be offered free via ads (Spongebob TD). -- [Roblox 2026-10 4:22](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=262) [Roblox 2026-10 6:23](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=383)
- **Respect platform price limits** [official, 2026-10-28, pricing]: Developer products 1 to 1,000,000,000 Robux. Paid access 25-1000 Robux. Models $2.99-$49.99, plugins $4.99-$249.99. Discounts don't apply above 1,000,000 Robux. Promoted on-sale passes must be 50-800 Robux. Paid private server price determines per-sale earnings. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux)
- **Complete metadata and visuals on every storefront surface** [official, 2026-10-28, thumbnails-icons]: Add images and videos to the game detail page (up to 10 per the docs) to improve conversion from impressions to plays. Complete title, description, schedule and thumbnails improve off-platform featuring odds. Use thumbnails and descriptions on your community group page. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)

- **Release on Friday, build momentum Wednesday** [official, 2023-09-19, updates-live-ops]: Launch on Fridays with dev cutoff Wednesday night or Thursday midday, when players are off work and school. Avoid Tuesdays. Build hype on Wednesday for momentum going into the weekend, which is hard to climb due to ad-heavy competition. Bad Business and Jailbreak used Friday releases. -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 15:37](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=937) [Roblox Creators 2022-02 14:31](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=871) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**

- **Audit collaborator changes with Activity History and Activity Tracker** [mixed, 2026-10-28, team-and-budget]: Use Activity History (Window menu in Studio or Creator Hub) to see who changed what and when, and filter by event type or collaborator when the log is large. - **Optimise CTR with thumbnails and test creatives** [devforum, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Older DevForum advice: stop ads under 1.0% CTR, aim for ~3.0%. MoseyTube's day-one benchmark is ~2.5% CTR and ~$0.008 cost per play. Split-test thumbnails, then shift budget to the winner. kra_DeZ tests 20+ AI thumbnails. - **Fund platform costs from wallet at the DevEx repurchase rate** [devforum, 2026-09-11, pricing]: Wallet holders can buy Robux at the DevEx rate of $0.0038/Robux to pay for publishing, testing and ads, instead of buying back at retail. Example: 10,000 Robux for $38; 30,000 Robux for $114, plus tax. This is a cost you pay, not your developer payout, so keep it separate when modeling net revenue. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- **Split early budget about 50:50 between development and ads, with a large emergency buffer** [devforum, 2022-08-14, team-and-budget]: When starting out, split the budget roughly 50:50 between development and ads and keep a large buffer for emergencies. -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Simple, quickly made creatives can beat expensive art** [devforum, 2023-12-31, thumbnails-icons]: Simple, eye-catching ad creatives made in Photoshop or Paint.NET can outperform expensive art on CTR, per the author. Prioritise legibility, bold colour and one subject over production budget. -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921) [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Keep the game actively maintained for Kids/Select eligibility** [devforum, 2026-05-11, updates-live-ops]: Roblox official guidance says regular updates and addressing user feedback speed up Kids/Select evaluation. Games with fewer than 100 highly engaged players in 60 days can lose eligibility. Promote to users aged 16+ to support evaluation. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944) [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ama-publishing-requirements-roblox-kids-select/4580953) **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: Kids/Select entry bar is 250 HEP in 60 days (cut from 500 on 2026-08-19); maintenance bar is 25 (cut from 100 on 2026-07-16); entry drops again to 100 in Nov 2026: CONFIRMED as announced by RDC26 staff post (2026-09-11) and 2026-10-12 publishing post, NOT live as of 2026-11-01 (docs still say 250) (Creator Docs kids-and-select + DevForum 'Highly Engaged Player Threshold Drops to 250' (staff, 2026-08-19), fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- **Split-test before big spend (launch test pattern)** [indie+devforum, 2026-10-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: DevForum 2020 pattern: split launch budget into three equal ads (clickbait, community, mobile sponsor), put daily spend on the highest-CTR for 3 days, archive the runner-up and re-run it later from revenue. kra_DeZ: ~30 credits/3 days for two thumbnails, then 90-100 credits over 4-5 days with the winner; schedule the next campaign to sta… -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805) [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:08](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=668)
- **Older Robux-era sponsored ad settings (bid, spread, budgets)** [indie+devforum, 2026-07-13, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: 2024 waveshine/DevForum: max bid ~0.01-0.02, unspent budget refunded; ~10,000 R$ over 10-15 days, 2-4 day intervals, abandon if bad at day 5-7. 2025 AlvinBlox: 10k-30k Robux over 2-4 weeks, not 100k. SmartyRBX seeded with 1,000 Robux. waveshine warns settings change; predates the 2025 Ads Manager. -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791) [waveshine 2024-10 4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242)
- **Fund ads with Robux or Wallet, and budget in ad credits** [indie+devforum, 2026-10-03, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Wallet funds can be spent directly on ads, in addition to Robux. Ad credits can be bought with Robux: about 1,000 Robux for roughly $3.80 of credits. Minimum credit purchase was 10 credits in 2025 (now 1 credit) (2,850 Robux) and minimum daily budget 5 in a 2025 campaign. Convert to Robux before judging cost. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505) [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636) **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 CONTRADICTED: docs now state minimum purchase or conversion is 1 ad credit (Creator Docs ads-manager, fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- **Reinvest earnings instead of cashing out early** [indie+devforum, 2025-07-04, business-and-payouts]: SmartyRBX, CallMeDogey and DevForum authors advise spending Robux on ads, devs and new projects. CallMeDogey cashed out about 2.3M Robux for about $8,000 and kept the rest to fund ads. Roblox's own 2018 advice is to scale spend only once ROI is positive. Cashing out converts at a loss compared with buying Robux. Avoid buying limited item… -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279) [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Monetise without spamming; use rewarded video well** [indie+devforum, 2026-10-02, discovery-algorithm]: Robux spend and spend days are signals. Non-spammy, non-pay-to-win monetization is favoured. Well-implemented rewarded video can lift spend and playtime. Suppress payers if Robux per user drops. Highly retentive games also get better ad distribution (lower CPP). -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921) [AlvinBlox 2025-06 7:46](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=466)
- **Structure ad creative tests as a matrix of variables** [indie, 2021-11-10, ab-testing-and-experiments]: Build a grid of creatives across durations and aspect ratios, for example 16 durations from 7.5s to 45s in 2.5s steps by 4 aspect ratios = 64 creatives. Modular builds (5s intro, 2.5s outro, content blocks) make the matrix cheap to produce. Indie, low-advice source. -- [GameMakers 2021-11 3:47](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=227) [GameMakers 2021-11 4:49](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=289) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Typical modern indie budget: ~$10/day for ~10 days** [indie, 2026-06-29, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: MoseyTube starts at $10/day for 10 days ($100), going to $15-20/day for competitive genres. Tizzy uses $16/day for at least 3 days (ideally a week; ~11 days/$180 to clear the threshold). CallMeDogey uses $5-8/day for 7-10+ days. Alex Builds Games used $16/day for 7 days (~$120). All self-reported. -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=60) [MoseyTube 2026-06 3:34](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=214)
- **Don't count on ads paying back immediately** [indie, 2026-01-05, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Self-reported results: TheDankDeveloper spent ~2,800 Robux for 2,654 visits and recovered ~1,200 Robux; an earlier ~3,135 Robux run lost ~2,953. ItsZyonic's ~$300 week-1 spend didn't retain the game. Aim for break-even monetisation and high CTR. -- [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688) [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 10:12](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=612)
- **Pay only while engaged players keep arriving, then stop at the target** [indie, 2026-10-23, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Yann ran ads 19 days at $20/day and said he should have stopped sooner. rasdev warns the new engagement goal is in beta and can overspend, so pause it manually once the engaged-player target is hit. -- [rasdev 2026-10 5:26](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=326) [Yann 2026-07 2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131)
- **Size the budget by game type and trend urgency** [indie, 2026-05-30, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Yann's three factors: trend urgency, whether the game needs multiplayer, and genre supply versus demand. Simulators need less spend than multiplayer games like tag or knockout. Niche genres like racing need more. -- [Yann 2026-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=163) [Yann 2026-05 3:25](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=205)
- **Track ad return in Robux, not just spend** [indie, 2026-08-30, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Duckable spent 37,000 Robux on ads and got about 10,000 back in the first 24-hour game. Yann spent 100,000 Robux (380 credits) over 30 days and got 30,000 back. Calculate return on every campaign before scaling. -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064) [Yann 2026-07 0:30](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=30)
- **Seed the algorithm with small consistent spend plus off-platform players** [indie, 2026-05-03, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: rasdev suggests 10k-30k Robux over 2-4 weeks of small, steady ad spend to generate data, not one big spend. Pair it with friends, dev communities, Discord, TikTok or YouTube. The goal is data the algorithm can act on. -- [rasdev 2026-05 7:45](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=465) [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121)
- **Pick run length and budget for a new game, then reassess** [indie, 2026-05-30, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: bubDEV sets a 250 lifetime budget (range 150-400) for 5-7 days on new games and says longer runs are unnecessary. Yann says reassess by 2 weeks. Duckable spent $16/day for 10 days and says a big budget is not required for a game to take off. -- [Yann 2026-05 7:30](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=450) [Duckable 2026-05 9:38](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=578)
- **Budget at a small scale first, scale only after proof** [indie, 2026-06-06, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: janisjanis moved from a 4,500 Robux first ad to a 10,000 Robux campaign only after early results, and that campaign brought the game 72 players and algorithm entry. Repeat small tests before major spend. -- [janisjanis01 2026-06 10:06](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=606) [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:26](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=746)
- **Check pacing in the first hours, not just the daily total** [indie, 2024-11-07, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Short bursts deliver most impressions at the start and die within minutes. Lifetime budgets can burn out in hours instead of spreading over the window (GnomeCode). Check spend and CCU within the first hour or two. GnomeCode's CPM settled at 0.08 credits per 1,000 impressions after that point. -- [GnomeCode 2024-11 3:05](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=185) [GnomeCode 2024-11 3:20](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=200) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Turn off auto-reload and cap spend with a daily budget** [indie, 2026-07-31, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Auto-reload can cause unplanned top-ups. Use a custom daily budget (7 to 12 credits/day were cited) and a fixed run length (LovJak used 10 credits/day for 3 days). Confirm both in Ads Manager before launch. -- [LovJak 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246) [LovJak 2026-07 2:41](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=161)
- **Plan for unused credits, which cannot be refunded to Robux** [indie, 2026-05-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: PolarBear left about half of the credits bought unused, and unused credits cannot be refunded to Robux. Buy in smaller batches and reconcile spend against credits. Cancelling early still recovers unused full days. -- [LovJak 2026-05 6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410) [LovJak 2026-05 8:55](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=535)
- **Bid per play above the 0.01 floor, usually 0.02 to 0.03** [indie, 2025-03-16, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Ad bids are cost-per-play. Below 0.01 the ad will not show, and higher bids win better auction placement. PolarBear used ~0.02 for small budgets and 0.05 to 0.06 above roughly 15,000 Robux. BubuTheDev says 0.01 to 0.03 is enough. -- [BubuTheDev 2025-03 2:04](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=124) [BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Schedule spend for peak days (weekends, Friday to Sunday)** [indie, 2026-05-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: PolarBear schedules sponsored campaigns for weekends when more players are online. LovJak targeted Friday to Sunday and started on a Friday at 4pm. Align run windows with your own concurrency data. -- [LovJak 2026-05 6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410) [PolarBear 2024-03 6:29](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=389)
- **Start geographically broad but narrow to your spenders** [indie, 2026-07-26, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: BubuTheDev starts with US and Canada, then narrows gender and age to the group that actually spends Robux (e.g. 13-17 or 18-24). Lnor's verified 18-24 PC and mobile targeting lifted CTR from ~1.5% to over 8% in a day. Test to confirm for your game. -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377) [BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82)
- **Verify that ad clicks can become plays** [indie, 2026-07-26, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Lnor found unverified users were clicking ads they could not enter, so the spend was wasted on clicks that could not become plays. Target verified players and confirm the join path works before launch. -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 4:55](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=295) [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377)
- **Run campaigns continuously; do not pull and repost** [indie, 2026-08-15, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Roblox spends about 24 hours learning after publish, and pulling and reposting resets that learning. RoyalDev's untouched ad drove ~8,000 visits versus ~1,000 to 4,000 when cycled on and off. Let it run before judging. -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163) [RoyalDev 2026-08 14:17](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=857)
- **Split large budgets across several days** [indie, 2026-05-10, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Mulch spread spend across days to test performance over time. Lnor warned against spending the whole budget at once after burning ~8,000 Robux untested. Spread spend and check results between tranches. -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122) [Mulch 2025-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=41)
- **Choose the destination experience before submitting** [indie, 2025-11-16, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: PolarBear says ads cannot be modified after submission, so the destination experience must be correct at creation. JustVeryYellow's walkthrough also picks the experience first, then objective, budget and targeting. -- [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 0:40](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=40) [PolarBear 2024-03 5:06](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=306)
- **Ask Roblox about co-marketing and UA support before funding off-platform acquisition alone** [indie, 2026-03-09, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Roblox reportedly offers on- and off-platform user acquisition support and co-marketing programs that can amplify a creator's off-platform acquisition spend. This is indie-reported (GamesBeat, 2026-03), so confirm eligibility and terms directly with Roblox before budgeting around it. -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- **Use paid ads to win back lapsed players, not only to acquire new ones** [indie, 2026-10-26, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Deconstructor of Fun reports a 40,000-player lapsed-player campaign (two shooter co-op campaigns) where 50% returned within one week at under $2 cost per activation. Claimed, not independently verified. Extends the existing 'Recent Players for lapsed ones' audience rule with a measured reactivation benchmark. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 37:13](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2233) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 36:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2192)
- **Use Discord quest ad buys for existing games as a reactivation channel** [indie, 2026-10-29, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Discord campaigns on existing games split roughly 50% new users and 50% lapsed reengagement, and about half the spend reactivates lapsed players. Treat this as a win-back channel alongside acquisition, not a pure new-user channel. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 39:51](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2391)
- **Scale user acquisition only when ROAS is positive** [indie, 2026-10-22, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Deconstructor of Fun warns that creator activations driving download uplift do not prove that paid ads are profitable. Only increase UA spend after measured return exceeds cost. Strengthens the existing 'Scale gradually when ROI is proven' rule. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 19:02](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1142)
- **Vary audience and avatar per product, as media buyers do** [indie, 2026-10-09, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Alex Builds Games advises treating each game as a media-buying test, varying audience and avatar per product to find winners. This suits multi-game publishers and differs from the existing 'broad targeting' default. Test a small set of audiences before committing budget. -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 13:01](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=781)
- **Expect most creatives to fail; produce 20-50 concepts per winner** [indie, 2026-02-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Across 25,000+ multivariate tests, 85-95% of new concepts failed to beat the best ad, and winners hit roughly 1 in 20 to 1 in 30. Size creative volume to monthly spend: too many wastes creatives, too few causes fatigue and running out. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86) [GameMakers 2022-03 6:39](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=399)
- **Keep a winning ad in its own campaign; never mix challengers into it** [indie, 2022-03-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: On Facebook, a champion's spend history cannot be removed, so a challenger added to the same campaign almost always loses. Run challengers in separate campaigns and compare them on equal footing. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Check placement watch time and completion, not just installs** [indie, 2021-11-10, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: A placement's skip rules determine how long viewers actually see the ad. Compare watch time and completion per placement alongside install rate before deciding where budget goes. -- [GameMakers 2021-11 12:45](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=765) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Do not use TikTok for influencer attribution without budgeting Spark Ads** [indie, 2026-06-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: TikTok influencer posts have no link-in-bio or sticker link, so attribution needs paid Spark Ads on top of the creator fee. That doubles the spend, so avoid TikTok when you need trackable installs. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056)
- **Make the game public before the first ad campaign** [indie, 2026-08-02, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Publish the game, then run the first campaign with goal Players, an all-player audience, auto-reload off, and 2-3 thumbnails at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days. -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324)
- **Step budget up to 16 ad credits per day only after the first campaign shows traction** [indie, 2026-08-02, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: After a first campaign at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days, launch a second at 16 ad credits per day for 2 days if traction shows. This is a controlled ladder, not a jump to full budget. -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365) [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324)
- **Stress-test ROAS targets against CPM swings** [indie, 2026-02-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: A hyper-casual example targeting about 110% ROAS over 30 days was broken by a 30% CPM drop. Model your return target with a downside CPM scenario before committing budget. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 14:59](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=899)
- **Expect paid installs to lag impressions and plan for more noise** [indie, 2026-02-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Paid installs rose about 10% while impressions rose about 20%, implying more noise per conversion. Use larger samples and confirm trends over several days before acting. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 22:21](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=1341)
- **Confirm no unintended duplicate campaigns run concurrently** [indie, 2025-06-15, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: One indie accidentally ran two launch ads at once, spending 5,000 Robux across both before the mistake was noticed. Before launch and after edits, list every active campaign and check for overlaps so spend and results stay attributable. -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:02](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=842) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Match ad mediation to the network you buy through** [indie, 2024-10-24, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Mediation via AppLovin MAX gives more granular AppLovin UA data, while IronSource mediation favours IronSource buying (anecdotal). Choose the mediation layer that matches the network you actually spend on. Mobile-app context, not Roblox. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 28:37](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1717) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Read the post-spike baseline, not the spike** [indie, 2026-10-17, analytics-metrics]: Ad pushes, updates and viral moments spike concurrency, then fade. Judge a change by where players settle about a week later. Duckable's CCU went 25 to 225 in two days, then dropped to near zero overnight. Compare settled baselines, not peaks. -- [rasdev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=319) [janisjanis01 2026-09 10:02](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=602)
- **Fix the game before buying more ads** [indie, 2026-05-30, analytics-metrics]: Check the overview benchmark stats first. Yann: if all six are above the 50th percentile on ads you are in good shape; if they are low, fix the game. janisjanis: low algorithm stats mean the game will not be pushed further regardless of budget. -- [Yann 2026-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408) [janisjanis01 2026-04 4:58](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=298)
- **Let ad campaigns clear the learning phase** [indie, 2026-10-11, analytics-metrics]: Day 1 of ads is a learning phase: do not change creatives or stop ads early. Watch error logs, funnel drop-offs and live players instead. Ad data takes up to 48 hours and plays can be credited up to 30 days later, so the first afternoon tells you almost nothing. -- [rasdev 2026-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674) [Yann 2026-05 4:46](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=286)
- **Track ad ROI as players and Robux against spend** [indie, 2026-07-05, analytics-metrics]: Compare visits, active players and Robux earned against ad spend. janisjanis's game (about 1 active player, about 1,000 lifetime Robux) did not cover its ad spend. Use the Traffic Sources and monetization tabs to attribute players and Robux to a promotion. -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730) [janisjanis01 2025-07 12:14](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=734)
- **Set a kill criterion for ad campaigns** [indie, 2026-08-26, analytics-metrics]: If no home recommendations appear after the ad run, cut the game and pivot rather than buying more ads. Do not buy the audience fee if home recs have not appeared by about day 5–8. Expect 1–2 weeks to reach 500 engaged players, since the counter updates about every 2 days. -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 7:30](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=450) [VuukStudios 2026-07 7:05](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=425)
- **Convert gross Robux to net before computing ad ROI** [indie, 2024-12-03, analytics-metrics]: Gross Robux overstates creator earnings. Mulch multiplies gross Robux by 0.7 before comparing to ad spend (low-advice; verify the current DevEx rate). BubuTheDev's ~2x return excluded conversion fees, so treat it as an upper bound. Compare Robux spent to Robux earned on the same basis. -- [BubuTheDev 2024-12 3:17](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=197) [PolarBear 2024-03 3:44](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=224) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Split new versus returning players after ad bursts** [indie, 2026-08-07, analytics-metrics]: After a paid burst, check average session time, DAU and returning versus new players. JustVeryYellow saw a spike fade about two days after ads ended. Visits versus unique players also reveal repeat behaviour (LovJak: 2K visits from 1.5K unique players). -- [LovJak 2026-08 8:55](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=535) [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 8:11](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=491)
- **Use a 7-day minimum click window for influencer attribution** [indie, 2026-06-10, analytics-metrics]: Standard UA uses a 24-hour window, which undercounts creator content that is watched for weeks or months. Use at least 7 days. Do not compare influencer results against UA on a 7-30 day window, because the comparison is unfair to creators. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 13:43](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=823) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659)
- **Judge creator campaigns on day-365 or lifetime installs, not day 7 or 30 CPI** [indie, 2026-06-10, analytics-metrics]: Tracking links keep converting for years. One client saw about 50% more installs at day 365 than at day 30 over two years of creator content. Plan budgets against long-tail numbers; Q1918 warns lifetime figures may come in well below planning assumptions. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741)
- **Expect delayed whale conversion from creator installs** [indie, 2026-06-01, analytics-metrics]: In a UK creator campaign, players installed about 15 days after publish but converted months later, often after 3-4 months. Short windows will miss this payer timing. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946)
- **Model ad performance when consent limits tracking** [indie, 2024-10-24, analytics-metrics]: With low opt-in (about 1 in 6 to 1 in 7 users in one mobile source), survey opted-in users and apply results to the wider cohort. Use one methodology across all channels so they compare fairly. Model at campaign and creative level, because blended ROAS alone cannot support granular budget decisions. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 15:31](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=931) [GameMakers 2024-10 21:02](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1262) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Build ad creatives from modular pre-rendered blocks** [indie, 2021-11-10, building-art-and-assets]: Use a standard intro and outro plus interchangeable content blocks. Duration can then be changed quickly by reordering or removing blocks instead of re-rendering the whole ad. Low-advice source; treat as a workflow idea. -- [GameMakers 2021-11 3:47](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=227) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Diversify income streams and fund big projects sustainably** [indie, 2025-11-15, business-and-payouts]: SmartyRBX: dev content, info products, assets, games, so one failure doesn't sink you. - **Evaluate buyouts; never give equity for advertising** [indie, 2026-09-08, business-and-payouts]: Studio buyers typically take at least 20% and often 40-50% of revenue. CallMeDogey says giving a percent for advertising or advice is a scam, since advertising is the easy part. - **Build a full P&L with creator rewards and publishing fees** [indie, 2026-08-30, business-and-payouts]: Track total revenue as Robux earned plus creator rewards. One creator ended at roughly -170,734 Robux net after these costs. -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 19:06](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1146) [janisjanis01 2026-05 11:50](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=710)
- **Budget UGC items as a first-year loss** [indie, 2026-07-04, business-and-payouts]: Creators reported losses early: one spent 31,150 Robux against 11,679 recovered, and reached break-even only after about one year (34,900 spent vs 35,966 earned). - **Run one paid UA campaign yourself to learn real margins** [indie, 2022-08-30, business-and-payouts]: Running your own user-acquisition campaign at least once shows how thin front-line ad profitability is. - **Don't hide paid progression or surprise players with monetisation** [indie, 2026-07-16, common-mistakes]: Hidden paid progression in a premium game caused a backlash that EA reversed within two days. - **Confirm traction before raising money or spending on user acquisition** [indie, 2025-02-04, development-process-and-speed]: Before soft launch, maximise the learning you get per dollar spent. ## 3. Objectives, targeting and placements (95)

- **Track channels and use deep links** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Create a separate share link per off-platform channel (no limit). Group-owned games need group-account links with permission. Use deep links/launchData for attribution and promo codes. Use launch data to tailor onboarding to ad audiences, spawn players at the ad's map or grant ad-only rewards; test the launch URL first (GetJoinData in on… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/deeplinks)
- **Meet in-experience ad technical specs** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Eligibility: public game, 13+ owner, ID verification, 2FA, 2,000 unique monthly visitors, approved maturity questionnaire (resubmitting temporarily removes eligibility). Billboards 8x4.5 to 32x18 studs; no second AdGui/SurfaceGui; only scale/position/rotation may change on portal ad packages. Impression criteria: image 1s, video 0.5s, 1.… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads)
- **Sponsored placement and dynamics** [official, 2026-10-12, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Moving sponsored rows higher on home gave 5x impressions with constant engagement. The row is now dynamic (row 2-10) and engaging games rank higher. 38% of the top 1,000 developers used sponsored experiences in Q2 2024; 71 of the top 100 run sponsored ads. Sponsored tiles lost effectiveness in 2022 after consolidation. -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 23:53](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1433) [Roblox Creators 2025-06 23:12](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1392)
- **Use empty targeting arrays deliberately** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: In targeting arrays (ageGroups, countries, devices), an empty array means all for that dimension. ageGroups options are AGE_13_17, AGE_18_24 and AGE_25_PLUS. Narrow only on purpose. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Publish state game rules clearly and fairly** [official, 2026-10-28, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: State game rules clearly where all users can see them, apply them fairly, and ensure rules comply with Community Standards. Ban messages may point to off-platform contact by brand or platform name, but must not include usernames, handles, or direct links. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players)
- **Treat off-platform links, free-Robux offers, and password requests as scams** [official, 2026-10-28, anti-exploit-and-security]: Never share your password, even with friends. Roblox staff never ask for passwords; report anyone who does via Report Abuse. Ignore free-Robux offers and lookalike domains such as rublux.com or r0blox.com. Verify claims against three trusted sources. Download Roblox only from official sources, and report vulnerabilities through Bug Bount… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/intro-to-digital-safety) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/roblox-account-creation)
- **Don't use fake urgency, pushy language, deceptive notifications or spam** [official, 2026-10-28, common-mistakes]: No inaccurate stock claims and no inaccurate or auto-restarting countdowns. With minors use 'View Item', 'See Price', 'Open Shop' instead of 'GET IT NOW'. No disguised ads, bait-and-switch rewards or pre-loaded purchases. Avoid purely informational notifications; prompt an action. Clickbait ads damage trust. Spamming ads, users, groups o… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications)
- **Answer the maturity questionnaire accurately and in order** [official, 2026-10-28, common-mistakes]: Base answers on the most mature content players can encounter. Don't add 17+ or Moderate/Restricted content before the label is granted. Any depicted blood is at least light; heavy blood is 17+; implied violence is mild; realistic consequences are moderate+. Inaccurate answers risk removal, 13+ restriction and moderation. Resubmitting re… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) [Roblox Creators 2021-12 16:48](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=1008)
- **Brand the group and link off-platform socials** [official, 2026-10-28, community-and-discord]: Name the community group after your studio so the group page reflects your brand. Add YouTube, Discord, or X social links to the community page so players can follow you off-platform. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion)
- **Use brand and partnership work for credibility and long-term presence** [official, 2023-09-19, mindset-and-strategy]: Define success upfront (reach and 80-85+ thumbs up for activations; revenue and users for licensed games). Early on brand projects build credibility. Get communication expectations in writing; pitch persistently. Experiences need seasonal updates or are just ads. -- [Roblox 2023-09 9:12](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=552) [Roblox Creators 2023-05 2:07](https://youtu.be/3Y4_B5-EHjY?t=127) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Offer reward-style items at 3-10 Robux value** [official, 2026-10-28, monetisation-devproducts]: For rewarded offers: boosters, currency, extra lives, temporary privileges worth about 3-10 Robux; not randomized items. Make them relevant, scaled to progression, limited/rare, and offered at strategic moments. Cap rewarded item value near 100x earnings per ad view and estimate cannibalisation conservatively. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads) [Roblox Creators 2025-08 22:35](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1355)
- **Use Roblox promotion and pricing tools** [official, 2026-10-28, monetisation-gamepasses]: Create a unique pass for the Buy Robux page; promoted passes must be priced above 49 and below 801 Robux, need a thumbnail, and can't grant paid random items. Promoted passes are free to users after a Robux purchase. Managed Pricing is on by default; disable per pass. Use price optimization (client-side scripts for personalized prices) o… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing)
- **Immersive ads (2023): portals, billboards, backfill** [official, 2026-10-28, monetisation-other]: Place portals where traffic is, not spawn or dead zones; portals earn ~10x image ads; use native backfill. Roblox holdouts found them neutral on engagement; teleporters mostly return within 24h. Conservative placement is under 40 teleports per 1000 hours. 2023 data; formats now listed as rewarded video, billboards, portals. -- [Roblox 2023-09 25:36](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1536) [Roblox 2023-09 28:35](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1715)
- **Ads API usage** [official, 2025-08-29, scripting-and-performance]: Call ad availability before showing the button and after each watch, close to when pressable; never show to ineligible users. -- [Roblox Creators 2025-08 24:40](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1480) [Roblox Creators 2025-08 25:21](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1521) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Use in-engine preview video and capture from spawn POV** [official, 2025-11-13, thumbnails-icons]: Gameplay preview video (3/month, reviewed within ~24h) autoplays before thumbnails; must be authentic with no CGI, UI overlays, off-platform footage; light post-processing OK. Older (2018) advice said trailers gave little lift. Spawn-POV camera positions yield free thumbnails. -- [Roblox Creators 2025-11 0:50](https://youtu.be/ag569uHM96E?t=50) [Roblox Creators 2025-11 1:51](https://youtu.be/ag569uHM96E?t=111)
- **Keep one icon per locale and support it with extra media** [official, 2026-10-28, thumbnails-icons]: Only one game icon is displayed per locale. Use supplementary image and video thumbnails to promote the game further alongside it. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/experience-icons)
- **Coordinate marketing and influencers** [official, 2023-09-19, updates-live-ops]: Line up YouTube, Twitter, group shouts and image updates to land together (Zoo Tycoon hit #2 on PC within 5-6 hours). Ask YouTubers for multi-part series (5 episodes over 5 weeks) paired with updates. Have a cohesive go-to-market plan including influencers. Adopt Me produces 10-12 video pieces per week. -- [Roblox 2023-09 30:49](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1849) [Roblox 2023-09 34:41](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=2081)
- **Use the Studio MCP server for external AI tools** [official, 2026-04-16, version-control-and-tooling]: Connect the Studio MCP server so external tools such as Cursor or Claude Code get the same Studio tools as the built-in Assistant. Use it instead of ad hoc scripts or copy-pasted instructions when an external agent needs to act on a Studio session. -- [Roblox 2026-04 39:20](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2360)
- **Upload creatives as Open Cloud image assets** [mixed, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Creatives must be Open Cloud image assets uploaded via the Assets API, then referenced by ID in creativeAssetIds. - **Make ads and icons viewable** [mixed, 2026-09-16, building-art-and-assets]: Image ads need to be viewable under IAB standards (most of the area visible for about a second), or earn nothing. Use Photo Booth for consistent item icons (stroke size 6). -- [Roblox 2023-09 28:51](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1731) [Roblox 2023-09 31:16](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1876)
- **Thumbnails, metadata and discovery checks; AI and localisation shortcuts** [mixed, 2026-10-28, common-mistakes]: Default thumbnails are ineligible for Home recommendations. Avoid ambiguous imagery and misleading metadata. Rejected thumbnails can cause bans. No social URLs or invite codes in-game. Tags alone don't drive Discovery. Ads don't penalise RFY. Games are not permanently quarantined. AI auto-translation overrides manual translations; AI mod… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)
- **Use groups and notifications** [mixed, 2026-10-12, community-and-discord]: Promote your Roblox group in-game (e.g. group-only item). Native community page announcements with polls notify members instantly; analytics show trends. kra_DeZ claims a group grew ~19k to 150k in weeks by sending update notifications (unverified). -- [Roblox 2026-10 6:11](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=371) [Roblox 2018-07 13:20](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=800)
- **Network by offering value** [mixed, 2026-09-14, community-and-discord]: Reach out to other developers (Twitter, cross-promotion); build many dev connections. - **Build a personal brand and culture** [mixed, 2025-08-15, community-and-discord]: Build culture around your name across Roblox, Discord and Twitter rather than marketing items; make a recognizable style signature rather than a name stamp. Let community help choose next styles. Creating dev content (devlogs, tutorials) or free resources builds an audience for commissions; post commission CTAs on X consistently. -- [Roblox Creators 2025-08 14:06](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=846) [Roblox Creators 2025-08 16:11](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=971) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Use fandom and characters; donate** [mixed, 2025-10-25, community-and-discord]: Strong characters and lore can build devoted fandoms even at small player counts. Monstercat's engagement campaign set release quantity. - **YouTubers, streamers and external traffic are strong seeds** [mixed, 2026-05-10, discovery-algorithm]: Roblox staff call a YouTuber pickup far and away the best way to get discovered (one case 200 to 4,000 CCU). - **Be patient with results, but cut losses on dead projects** [mixed, 2026-10-20, mindset-and-strategy]: Some games or items take a week or months to take off; growth can lag campaigns (Spin or Die ~4 months after ads). - **Do not paywall progression to chase Roblox promotion** [mixed, 2026-10-28, monetisation-gamepasses]: One creator claims paywalled progression and addictive loops earn front-page placement. - **Publishing fee waiver and ad credits** [mixed, 2026-10-28, monetisation-other]: Keeping Roblox Plus or Premium 2 consecutive months before publishing for all ages skips the fee. - **Design clippable, chaotic, surprising moments** [mixed, 2026-07-24, social-features-and-virality]: Short, bright loops; funny PvP drama (stealing cash, ragdolling thieves); unexpected or secret content (Bigfoot/unicorn in a random run) drive virality and free YouTuber coverage. - **Use a Discord bot to manage group members from chat** [devforum, 2017-10-25, community-and-discord]: A bot can promote, demote, and announce to group members, letting clan leaders run admin tasks from chat. - **Track Share Link performance in Creator Analytics** [devforum, 2025-06-24, social-features-and-virality]: Creator Analytics compares Share Link visits with resulting engagement and earnings. - **Scale tester pay by revenue, bug volume and tester type** [devforum, 2023-02-26, team-and-budget]: Do not pay more experienced testers more within the same QA program. Advertise tester openings publicly rather than targeting individuals, and assess inexperienced testers via practice tests on a public version. -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Create a conceptual art summary and reuse it everywhere** [devforum, 2019-06-02, thumbnails-icons]: Make one piece of concept art that sums up the game's ideas, context, style and art direction. - **Put the Details Page URL in every promo post** [devforum, 2026-09-11, trailers-and-video-marketing]: Share the game's Details Page URL in YouTube descriptions, X posts, and Discord so players can start playing in Chrome without installing (Play on the Web, announced for late 2026). - **Verify audience age and targeting quality** [indie+devforum, 2026-06-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Roblox serves ads only to 13+ (Tizzy says ads automatically reach 16+). ItsZyonic found the sponsored audience skewed under-18 with many lying about age (2023 advice: 17+ for best return). Pick phone vs PC to match the audience. -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279) [ItsZyonic 2023-06 2:17](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=137)
- **Gate privileged actions with server checks and least privilege** [indie+devforum, 2025-12-03, anti-exploit-and-security]: Compare player.UserId on the server before firing any admin-only action, since client checks are not secure. Cap the maximum promotable group rank so bot-driven promotions cannot reach officer ranks. Grant the Monetize Experience permission only to roles that manage gamepasses and subscriptions, separate from Edit and Publish. -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/managing-your-group-revenue-and-experience-ownerships-just-became-a-lot-easier/4114133) [Roblox DevForum 2017-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201)
- **Pitch bigger YouTubers by business email; start small for free playthroughs** [indie+devforum, 2023-08-18, community-and-discord]: Send promotion pitches to larger YouTubers' business email rather than informal channels; TheDevKing says this helped the launch. - **Avoid content that breaks official video guidelines** [indie+devforum, 2026-10-23, trailers-and-video-marketing]: Do not use cinematic trailers, dramatized gameplay, promo text, real-life footage, voiceovers, narration, or music with lyrics in Roblox game-page videos. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gameplay-videos-on-home-help-your-games-get-discovered/4842601) [rasdev 2026-10 3:24](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=204)

- **Expect a slow ramp while the algorithm learns the audience** [indie, 2026-10-23, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: tef says the ad algorithm needs time to learn who wants to play the game before it pushes to the right players. janisjanis reported a surge after a scaled campaign, so ramp timing varies. - **Check age rules for European audiences** [indie, 2025-11-16, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Ads targeting European regions require accounts aged 13+ due to local laws. This was a 2021 invitation, not a self-serve option. -- [Last Level Studios 2021-08 12:24](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=744) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Don't credit a traffic spike to ads before checking for events** [indie, 2026-08-20, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: A StudioKrew agency case (Speed Keyboard Escape, Aug 2026) attributes its record spikes to admin abuse events, not ads. Check the event calendar, admin actions and promotions before reading a CCU or visit spike as ad performance. - **Vet local agencies through case studies** [indie, 2026-06-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Agencies know local creators and often get better rates, and they reduce coordination overhead for small teams. Judge them on case studies before signing. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 31:30](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1890)
- **Measure creator marketing on performance, not vanity metrics** [indie, 2024-10-24, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Creator marketing on PC and console is growing but measured poorly. - **Use Social Links instead of bypassing moderation to advertise** [indie, 2019-09-06, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: Do not try to get around Roblox moderation to promote external links in chat or descriptions. - **Judge promotional content by every surface it appears on** [indie, 2022-08-26, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: Content acceptable inside an experience can still violate rules on another surface. - **Plan for age-gated audience reach at launch** [indie, 2026-07-26, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: If the game is built for kids, the age-gated audience limits how widely you can advertise. - **Check giveaway rules per country before launching** [indie, 2026-06-01, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: Some countries restrict giveaways of tangible prizes, so get legal review per target market before launch. - **Use stage-gated playtime targets** [indie, 2026-08-16, analytics-metrics]: Judge average playtime against the stage the game is in: aim for 10+ min during ad-driven traffic and 20+ min once on the algorithm. - **Optimise campaigns on a frequent event when revenue is sparse** [indie, 2024-10-24, analytics-metrics]: When revenue events fire rarely, choose a frequently firing optimisation event (for example tutorial complete or a CPA-style action) as the campaign target. - **Know that brands pay for placements and events** [indie, 2024-08-30, brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]: Brands pay for in-game advertising and event placements. - **Build a pre-release audience on Instagram and TikTok** [indie, 2026-08-09, community-and-discord]: One 2026 creator grew Instagram videos to millions of views during development. - **Offer a clear fantasy; weigh monetisation against engagement** [indie, 2026-10-28, core-loops-and-game-design]: Answer why do I care, what is the setting and what am I grinding at; RPGs need an objective from the start.
