Engagement first; pay-to-win irritates older players; give earned-currency alternatives to Robux; never require Robux for missions; place rewarded ads at natural breaks. -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 8:05](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=485) [VuukStudios 2026-09 11:34](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=694)
- **Split the build into ordered objectives** [indie, 2026-10-26, development-process-and-speed]: Work through milestones in order, e.g. map, then pets or units, then animations, menus, monetisation, beta and ads. Build the place, then core features, then layer everything else. -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 1:21](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=81) [MonarchDev 2026-10 1:04](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=64)
- **Combine two or three inspirations instead of cloning one** [indie, 2026-10-28, game-ideas-and-genres]: rasdev says 'steal like an artist': merge 2-3 sources, including non-game ones (movies, TikTok), into one easy-to-understand idea. Examples: tennis plus tower, fighting plus RPG, Rematch plus Mario Kart items, RNG roll plus tower defense, Dead Rails (history, trains, werewolves), Animal Hospital (mix of three hits). Roblox official also… -- [rasdev 2026-09 0:49](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=49) [rasdev 2026-06 1:51](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=111)
- **Avoid genres dominated by studios or with high quality bars** [indie, 2026-10-11, game-ideas-and-genres]: Don't solo anime/Battlegrounds as a beginner. Realistic FPS has a small audience and expensive ads. Plain RPGs are hard to monetise (1_Bruce). Horror and RPG have less competition at the top but high bars and long build times (rasdev). Kids beat scary horror once and leave (kra_DeZ). Roblox lists horror as growing, so the sources conflic… -- [rasdev 2026-06 3:26](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=206) [1_Bruce 2026-04 2:06](https://youtu.be/iGng-GC_xFk?t=126)
- **Vary enemy behaviours and scale PvE difficulty** [indie, 2026-09-13, genre-mechanics]: Single-note enemies lead to kiting into an AoE clump. Flat difficulty plus player power growth makes late game trivial; ramp difficulty across campaign stages. -- [Cumin 2026-09 8:11](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=491) [Cumin 2025-07 11:43](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=703)
- **Make the first pitch self-contained** [indie, 2026-02-07, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: Include screenshots, a trailer and a direct playable link in the first message so the creator sees what the game is and can play immediately. Never send a bare 'play my game' DM. -- [Cumin 2026-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=41)
- **Do pre-launch marketing and hype** [indie, 2025-12-11, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: Lnor's big update launched with only about three players because no marketing was done. bubDEV says pre-release hype on YouTube, TikTok or Instagram brings a loyal base that gives the algorithm early engagement. -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 6:46](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=406) [bubDEV 2025-12 10:17](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=617)
- **Negotiate reuse rights for creator cutdowns and short-form clips** [indie, 2026-10-22, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: When booking a creator activation, ask for cutdowns and short-form clips you can reuse in your own lower-funnel UA ads. Per the source, that reuse, not the single creator video, is the main value. Self-reported indie advice; confirm usage terms in writing. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:39](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1059)
- **Think like a business owner / CEO guiding the artist** [indie, 2025-07-21, mindset-and-strategy]: SmartyRBX: treat games as products meeting market needs, set tangible goals (earnings, players), plan a career timeline, use strategy for concept and marketing and passion for components (models, assets, UI). Build with the market in mind or it ignores you. He says 99% of devs should not follow passion first (unverified claim). -- [SmartyRBX 2025-06 21:47](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=1307) [SmartyRBX 2025-06 25:54](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=1554)
- **Check visibility before cutting pass prices** [indie, 2025-06-26, monetisation-gamepasses]: A 50 to 25 Robux price cut during a campaign did not fix low earnings. One creator concluded that monetisation was too subtle in-game. Fix placement and prominence before lowering prices. -- [Mulch 2025-06 2:45](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=165) [Mulch 2025-06 2:04](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=124)
- **Expect very small early Robux revenue** [indie, 2025-10-11, monetisation-other]: New games can earn very little before algorithm reach. One creator recorded over 1,000 Robux first revenue before reaching the algorithm, 31 Robux in 72 hours, and 230 Robux total after ads ran. Plan cash flow and runway accordingly. -- [janisjanis01 2025-10 16:18](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=978) [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:31](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=751) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Apply the Xbox test before shipping** [indie, 2023-08-25, player-psychology-and-game-feel]: Ask whether a kid's friends would keep watching the gameplay rather than scrolling TikTok. If not, the game doesn't look fun enough. Make the game look and feel fun enough that friends won't reach for their phone. -- [1Coal 2023-08 1:04](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=64)
- **Fix save and core-loop bugs before a traffic spike** [indie, 2020-02-14, playtesting-and-feedback]: A front-page surge with broken saving or empty plots lost most new players at Bakery Tycoon's 2020 launch, which peaked at 20,000 CCU. Before promotion or featuring, run a full save-and-reload cycle and confirm core progression works for new accounts. -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 11:47](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=707) [Last Level Studios 2020-02 11:05](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=665)
- **Run a half-price first week to read real conversion and ARPPU** [indie, 2026-05-30, pricing]: Yann advises selling gamepasses and products at roughly half price in the first week to get a realistic payer conversion rate and ARPPU from ad traffic. Use it as a measurement window, then adjust toward the target price. Self-reported; no benchmark figures given. -- [Yann 2026-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408)
- **Plan for the 16+ gate on new games (since May 19, 2026)** [indie, 2026-06-30, roblox-platform-changes]: New games are shown only to 16+ users until they reach 500 highly engaged players. Plan launch audience, marketing and external traffic around this gate. Age-rule changes can sharply cut reachable audience (indie-reported). -- [Tizzy 2026-06 2:39](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=159) [Raul 2026-06 2:14](https://youtu.be/vnpCimQkHoQ?t=134) **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: Kids/Select entry bar is 250 HEP in 60 days (cut from 500 on 2026-08-19); maintenance bar is 25 (cut from 100 on 2026-07-16); entry drops again to 100 in Nov 2026: CONFIRMED as announced by RDC26 staff post (2026-09-11) and 2026-10-12 publishing post, NOT live as of 2026-11-01 (docs still say 250) (Creator Docs kids-and-select + DevForum 'Highly Engaged Player Threshold Drops to 250' (staff, 2026-08-19), fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- **Repurpose short clips into long-form** [indie, 2025-11-02, social-features-and-virality]: A TikTok format turned into a long YouTube video took off almost instantly (Creators on the Blox, low-advice video). -- [Creators on the Blox 2025-11 9:32](https://youtu.be/f9BmInLHotA?t=572)
- **Get popular Roblox YouTubers to cover the game early** [indie, 2020-09-17, social-features-and-virality]: Emancyphur credits early coverage from popular Roblox YouTubers with large player growth and no paid ads. Pitch a playable build to large channels before launch and support streamers who cover the game. This partly conflicts with the 'smaller creators first' rule; see disagreements. -- [Emancyphur 2020-09 2:50](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=170) [Emancyphur 2020-09 11:17](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=677) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Cross-post devlog clips to Instagram Reels** [indie, 2026-08-09, social-features-and-virality]: During development, studio rook's TikTok videos averaged about 2 views, while Instagram pushed devlog videos to millions. A devlog posted with reels reached 501 views against a ~100 expectation. Test Instagram Reels alongside TikTok for devlogs. -- [studio rook. 2026-08 1:24](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=84) [studio rook. 2026-06 0:01](https://youtu.be/WCIBNClvUrQ?t=1)
- **Use a link-gated skill leaderboard giveaway for attribution** [indie, 2026-06-01, social-features-and-virality]: Run a 1-2 week in-app skill leaderboard giveaway that only players who click the creator's link can enter. The gate ties engagement to a specific promoter, so you can see which creator or channel drove players. Check Roblox giveaway and prize rules before launch. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 18:27](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1107)
- **Start structured A/B tests once a title is stably earning** [indie, 2025-02-04, studio-business-funding-and-publishing]: GameMakers (indie, self-reported) places the mid-stage at roughly a couple hundred thousand per year in revenue, where a stable title is making money. At that point, shift from ad-hoc changes to structured A/B tests. Treat the threshold as a rough guide, not a fixed rule. -- [GameMakers 2025-02 14:21](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=861) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Delay marketing hires until soft-launch validation** [indie, 2024-10-24, team-and-budget]: Before proof of concept, hire at most one internal marketing person, or use a trusted aligned partner. Avoid building a large team before soft launch. The validation bar cited is only a few thousand users through the funnel, so reach that first. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 35:44](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2144) [GameMakers 2024-10 37:46](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2266)
- **Use CTR benchmarks** [indie, 2026-10-20, thumbnails-icons]: Ad CTR: under 1–1.5% drop the thumbnail, ~2.5% average, 3.5%+ exceptional (kra_DeZ ~4%). CallMeDogey's small-dev floor is 0.7%, peak nearly 3%. Zyonic reported 1.5% with a clickbait icon. All self-reported. -- [kra_DeZ 2026-10 3:31](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=211) [CallMeDogey 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/SjjPGduApZU?t=0)
- **For sponsored ads, prefer 16:9 thumbnails over icons** [indie, 2026-10-23, thumbnails-icons]: Sponsored ads that switched from square icons to 16:9 thumbnails reported about 40% higher playthrough. The 16:9 image is the larger image in search results, so it matters more than the small icon. -- [rasdev 2026-10 5:39](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=339) [rasdev 2026-10 4:04](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=244)
- **Build vertical creatives on a matching 800x1000 canvas** [indie, 2025-10-18, thumbnails-icons]: For vertical TikTok-style video, create the composite at 800x1000 and keep that canvas size when pasting, so images are not stretched when the video is rendered vertically. -- [#7Phantom 2025-10 3:42](https://youtu.be/sByQYCWWlwE?t=222) [#7Phantom 2025-07 6:51](https://youtu.be/xjZXsHxKerE?t=411)
- **Log the best thumbnail CTR against the ad creative CTR** [indie, 2025-07-13, thumbnails-icons]: In one indie test, the best-performing thumbnail reached about 3% CTR while the ad creative reached about 1.9%. Record both numbers per test so you can see whether thumbnail packaging beats the baseline creative. -- [janisjanis01 2025-07 16:47](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=1007) [janisjanis01 2025-07 17:26](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=1046) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Define the target audience before choosing the title and posts** [indie, 2023-02-04, titles-descriptions]: Decide the audience first (kids, teens, etc.), then word the title and marketing posts around what that audience cares about. This sequencing is new; the existing audience rule covers differentiating same-genre games, not choosing the audience before naming and posting. -- [SmartyRBX 2023-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/Wh2B4cQn4Zs?t=0)
- **Match content language to core audience** [indie, 2026-10-11, trailers-and-video-marketing]: VuukStudios' TikTok is mostly Spanish because that is the playerbase. -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 7:17](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=437) [VuukStudios 2026-08 8:45](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=525)
- **Check off-platform demand** [indie, 2026-06-29, trend-research-and-validation]: Steam wishlists, CrazyGames votes and views, YouTube coverage, and 'where can I play this' comments show unmet demand. Check whether content creators have covered the game. -- [Tizzy 2026-06 1:42](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=102) [Tizzy 2026-05 4:37](https://youtu.be/8q6kdFVL_fY?t=277)
- **Ride cultural and social trends** [indie, 2026-08-26, trend-research-and-validation]: Use TikTok, Shorts and Reels trends and memes (brainrot, challenges) and check the trend's cause because many are just news. Interpret outside pop culture for item spikes. Fuse a proven concept with a meme or character. Social arbitrage: build around real-life trends. -- [Tizzy 2026-05 8:53](https://youtu.be/8q6kdFVL_fY?t=533) [CaberJaber 2026-03 2:46](https://youtu.be/XEA41eXTfoA?t=166)
- **Learn from accelerator and proven teams** [indie, 2025-08-24, trend-research-and-validation]: Watch teams from Roblox's accelerator and study YouTube channels with proven formats. Copy formats for Shorts promotion. -- [janisjanis01 2025-08 9:06](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=546) [1Coal 2023-08 3:16](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=196)
- **Save competitor and non-genre ads on your own device** [indie, 2022-03-01, trend-research-and-validation]: Save competitor and adjacent-genre ads regularly. Ad networks serve more ads to users who actively click and install, so a heavily used feed skews what you see. Keep a clean, separate browsing setup for research. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 24:53](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1493) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Judge ad creatives against a ~3% CTR target** [indie, 2025-06-15, trend-research-and-validation]: janisjanis01 found ads at ~2% click-through were not enough and aimed for ~3%. Track CTR per ad creative and treat anything near 2% as underperforming. This complements the existing falling-CTR saturation signal. -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 13:58](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Gate continue-or-abandon decisions on traction** [indie, 2026-09-23, updates-live-ops]: Once a game shows traction (for example home recommendations), update weekly, or bi-weekly for bigger titles. If it shows no traction after the post-release window, stop and start a new project. Matt The Method reports earnings collapsed after updates and promotion stopped, so keep updating and promoting while the game has traction. -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 13:08](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=788) [VuukStudios 2026-08 10:55](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=655)

## 4. Creatives and testing (40)

- **Test reward value against perceived cost when ad impressions per DAU are low** [official, 2026-10-30, ab-testing-and-experiments]: Experiment with reward size relative to the perceived cost of watching. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-optimization)
- **Start Open Cloud with an API key, test in Postman, then scope down** [official, 2026-10-28, ai-and-tools]: API keys are the easiest start; use OAuth 2.0 for user-authorized apps. Test endpoints in Postman with the OpenAPI descriptions first. Send the key in the x-api-key header and grant only needed scopes (for ads: ad.campaign:read, ad.campaign:write, ad.billing:read). -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Run many ad creatives to find the highest-CTR thumbnail** [official, 2026-10-20, thumbnails-icons]: Ad limits grew over time: 5 per campaign (2025-06), 10 (2025-10/2026-02), Tizzy cites 25 (2026-07), kra_DeZ says max about 15. Traffic splits evenly. Advice ranges from 2–3 up to 10–20+ variants; more is better. Then set the winner as main thumbnail. -- [Roblox Creators 2026-02 2:17](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=137) [Roblox Creators 2025-10 19:08](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1148)
- **Add rewarded video to monetise non-payers** [mixed, 2026-10-12, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Opt-in video (6-30s, 6-15 recommended, full-screen horizontal, 13+). Eligibility moved from 100K DAU (2025) to 2,000 unique monthly visitors with a public unrestricted game and 13+ creator (Nov 2025). Reported: 87% favourability, 1,700+ games, 27% of integrators earning >10% from it. Use Studio plugin templates, a test universe, and mult… -- [Roblox 2026-10 9:38](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=578) [Roblox 2026-10 11:02](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=662)
- **Judge ads by play-through rate and CPP, not clicks** [mixed, 2026-10-28, analytics-metrics]: Track CTR (clicks/impressions), CPC, conversion (attributed purchases/impressions) and CPP (Robux spent / attributed purchases) for sponsored items. Read clicks against actual plays, since they can diverge, and use attribution of 1-day view and 28-day click-through. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items) [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597)
- **Build a Discord/group community before launch** [mixed, 2025-04-28, community-and-discord]: Roblox says initial users are very hard to attract without community support; start about a month before release, not much earlier. AlvinBlox: create a Discord or group first and invite friends/testers, then grow slowly (10 to 50 to 100 players) before scaling marketing. -- [Roblox Creators 2024-08 23:34](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1414) [AlvinBlox 2025-04 2:00](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=120)
- **Design for clippability and work with content creators** [mixed, 2026-07-28, community-and-discord]: Ask what moments a creator would film and build them into the core loop; watch creator videos to see which moments they show. Build an influencer program rewarding creators who link/credit your game. Nurture a recognized community creator. -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 36:26](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=2186) [Tizzy 2026-07 16:30](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=990)
- **Playtest every update before shipping** [mixed, 2026-08-05, playtesting-and-feedback]: Test every release, even furniture-only ones (access, availability, price). Never ship last-second updates. Run a tester team before big updates, and use community betas or paid testing for larger ones. Playtest before paid ads, and check group-gated access works for members. -- [Roblox 2024-09 22:31](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1351) [Roblox Creators 2022-03 43:40](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2620)
- **Collect feedback through ongoing channels** [mixed, 2026-10-14, playtesting-and-feedback]: Use Discord bug reports, group forums, the creator dashboard Feedback tab AI summary, in-game mailboxes with AI theme summaries, social networks, event follow-ups, DevForum and TikTok livestreams. Reply to reviews when fixed. Watch small kid-recorded videos. -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 0:47](https://youtu.be/e9II73see-8?t=47) [Roblox Creators 2021-12 18:32](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=1112)
- **Limit video edits to real mechanics** [mixed, 2026-10-28, thumbnails-icons]: Camera work, minor post-processing, logo overlays and curated highlight sequences are allowed if they show real mechanics. Cinematic trailers, over-enhanced graphics, voiceovers, narration, lyric music and promo text are not allowed. Misrepresented videos are rejected and still count against the quota. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails) [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571)
- **Test names before committing** [mixed, 2026-10-12, titles-descriptions]: Test names with multiple game copies and ads teleporting players into the main game. Keep a big idea list and have trusted friends choose the most clickable title. -- [Roblox 2026-10 31:38](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=1898) [Creators on the Blox 2025-10 17:41](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=1061)
- **Pre-roll video ads need no code** [devforum, 2026-09-11, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Official: pre-roll video ads (coming soon) need no code. Roblox optimises delivery and shares revenue. Roblox Plus subscribers won't see them. Plan for pre-roll as a passive revenue source, not a build task, and check rollout status before depending on it. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- **Treat correct ProcessReceipt as a launch gate** [devforum, 2026-06-24, monetisation-devproducts]: Per the DevForum post, correct ProcessReceipt handling is required for eligibility for external surfacing and ensures purchases are delivered immediately. Finish and test it before expecting external promotion. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505)
- **Use player-initiated rewarded video only** [devforum, 2026-08-25, monetisation-other]: Rewarded Video Ads are exempt from feed-style restrictions: the player must start the ad and Roblox rate-limits views. Use these instead of automatic reward loops. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188)
- **Create the reward dev product in the same universe** [devforum, 2025-07-24, scripting-and-performance]: For rewarded ads, create the reward developer product in the same universe as the place. The server calls CreateAdRewardFromDevProductId and ShowRewardedVideoAdAsync, then grants the reward in ProcessReceipt. -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Build a persona-by-trend concept matrix before producing ads** [indie, 2022-03-01, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Segment players into personas by motivation (e.g. excitement, success, relaxation). Cross these rows with competitor creative trends as columns. Each cell is one concept with a defined intent, so ads speak to a specific motivation rather than a vague one. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 5:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=358) [GameMakers 2022-03 11:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=712) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Test fast and judge creatives on CTR and installs per impression (IPM)** [indie, 2025-11-23, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Do not over-polish. A rough, wacky ad can beat a careful one. Rank each creative on click-through rate and IPM (impressions per install) before scaling it. -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856) [GameMakers 2022-03 26:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1618)
- **Exclude your own test purchases from sales data** [indie, 2026-05-13, analytics-metrics]: Test purchases inflate developer product totals. ThePurpleGuy removed two self-tests from his totals. Filter them out before judging whether a product or ad test paid back. -- [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 1:22](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=82)
- **Playtest, test exploits and progression before launch** [indie, 2026-10-23, common-mistakes]: Repeat actions (respawning cows, tapping for farming) and check stacking limits; one player stacked hundreds of turrets on one tile. Patches can create new exploits: a punishing spawn was itself farmed. Playtest and balance early, walk progression to the end for impossible points (a wave-76 wall came from a missing config), and test mult… -- [tef 2026-10 13:25](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=805) [janisjanis01 2026-08 16:23](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=983)
- **Don't embed external video or social feeds in-game** [indie, 2026-08-25, common-mistakes]: Roblox only allows links from an approved list. Embedding external video or social feeds, or sharing off-platform, can get a game pulled. Check the current approved list before adding any external feed. -- [eGamers 2026-08](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/)
- **Creator videos can beat paid Roblox ads, but ads still help** [indie, 2026-10-11, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: janisjanis01 judged creator videos a better player source than paid Roblox ads. VuukStudios advises running ads on every major update, especially weekends. Games at 5-10K CCU can be revived by ads (one went idle to ~4K CCU, self-reported). These are not mutually exclusive. -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 2:06](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=126) [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 2:49](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=169)
- **Always-on in core countries, blitz-test new markets** [indie, 2026-06-10, influencer-and-creator-marketing]: Keep always-on campaigns in a few core countries and test new markets with different creators and verticals. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 5:31](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=331)
- **Diversify channels and income streams** [indie, 2026-10-03, mindset-and-strategy]: Stack several channels at once, such as paid ads plus short-form video; one creator saw compounding results. Build multiple income streams (games, commissions, store packs) rather than relying on one product. -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 14:02](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=842) [Erthlord 2026-01 14:45](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=885)
- **Test a game yourself on mobile before investing in it** [indie, 2020-05-27, playtesting-and-feedback]: Before agreeing to fund or invest in another developer's game, play it thoroughly yourself, including on mobile. Judge the actual experience, not the pitch or trailer. -- [TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617)
- **Have non-developers play and log funnel friction** [indie, 2024-10-24, playtesting-and-feedback]: Ask the marketing team to play the app and record funnel friction, screenshots and video. This surfaces onboarding blockers and broken tracking, such as a faulty analytics SDK implementation, that developers may miss. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 44:27](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2667)
- **Edit TikTok videos in the in-built editor** [indie, 2026-01-18, social-features-and-virality]: janisjanis reported externally edited videos got an unoriginal/low-quality flag that blocked For You eligibility. Use TikTok's built-in editor for promotional clips. This is a single creator's report and is low-advice. -- [janisjanis01 2026-01 6:01](https://youtu.be/IWzAHgHPhhU?t=361)
- **Favor YouTube for item promo videos, based on the latest data** [indie, 2026-07-23, social-features-and-virality]: In Erthlord's July 2026 data, YouTube drove about 108,000 views for item promos while TikTok drove under 5,000. An earlier January 2026 reel gave TikTok more views (17,000+ vs 6,000 YouTube). Test per channel before committing. -- [Erthlord 2026-07 14:46](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=886) [Erthlord 2026-01 9:11](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=551)
- **Organise roles into three functions: creative, UA operations, data/modeling** [indie, 2024-10-24, team-and-budget]: Structure the team around creative, user acquisition operations, and data/modeling. Automate data work with tooling rather than hiring for it, because dashboards and models are repeated across games and become redundant. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 31:37](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1897)
- **Prepare two thumbnails plus an icon for ad A/B tests** [indie, 2025-11-06, thumbnails-icons]: Build two candidate thumbnails and one icon before launching ads. The icon can double as a third thumbnail candidate, so you have three creatives to compare. -- [VuukStudios 2025-11 7:45](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=465)
- **Use a thumbnail generator tool to produce testable variants** [indie, 2026-07-29, thumbnails-icons]: RoThumb's was cited for generating thumbnails cheaper than commissioning artists. One creator reported about 5% CTR on own games and about 10% on a friend's game. Another doubted the clicks, so verify each output with a real test ad. -- [VuukStudios 2026-07 8:02](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=482) [Raul 2026-07 3:44](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=224)
- **Generate a colour palette first and use it as the baseline** [indie, 2026-10-26, thumbnails-icons]: Create a palette before other visual work, for example with ChatGPT, then reuse it for thumbnails, icons, UI and marketing. This keeps the look consistent across assets. -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 3:18](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=198)
- **Expect no in-place thumbnail swap on a live ad** [indie, 2026-08-06, thumbnails-icons]: Roblox does not let you replace the thumbnail on an active ad. To test a new thumbnail, launch a new ad creative and disable the old one. -- [Raul 2026-08 4:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=244)
- **Put the game name in short-form video captions** [indie, 2026-01-23, titles-descriptions]: Include the exact game name in captions on short-form video (TikTok, Shorts, Reels) so viewers can search for it and jump straight to play. Self-reported indie tactic; no conversion figures were given. -- [Erthlord 2026-01 2:14](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=134)
- **Structure external trailers: hook, features, title card with CTA** [indie, 2026-09-01, trailers-and-video-marketing]: rasdev: 60-90s total; 5-10s high-action hook; 15-20s core features and map; then combat/other features; end with title and call to action; cut music on beat. Note this is for off-platform trailers; on-platform videos have stricter guideline limits (no promo text, etc.), so the CTA/title card applies to external use. -- [rasdev 2026-09 10:07](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=607) [rasdev 2026-08 11:31](https://youtu.be/MH9TaOMTm9Q?t=691)
- **Find your over-1,000-view pattern and repeat it** [indie, 2025-12-22, trailers-and-video-marketing]: Track which videos exceed 1,000 views, identify the common element (e.g. a clothing set), and repeat. Post a new slideshow every couple of weeks featuring current best sellers. Reported (unverified) TikTok slideshow 4M views. -- [#7Phantom 2025-12 0:59](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=59) [#7Phantom 2025-12 1:26](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=86)
- **Platform choice: TikTok first, short-form as acquisition** [indie, 2026-08-26, trailers-and-video-marketing]: TheDevBacon calls TikTok's algorithm extraordinary; Matt The Method says ranting/short videos worked for acquisition (low-advice). Use channel-level promo of an existing game if available. -- [Matt The Method 2026-08 3:17](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=197) [janisjanis01 2025-06 12:16](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=736)
- **Time creator videos 1-2 weeks before launch** [indie, 2026-08-10, trailers-and-video-marketing]: Get creators to make videos a week or two before launch. Start trailers earlier than other promo, and go to full promo speed only near release. Indie, self-reported. -- [Cumin 2026-08 20:20](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1220)
- **Build a durable style guide for brand assets** [indie, 2026-06-10, trailers-and-video-marketing]: Create a style guide covering logos, lockup, and character art. A durable guide can stay in use for years, so its production cost is spread over time. Apply it consistently across trailers, thumbnails, and Shorts. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 13:42](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=822)
- **Track about 10 direct competitors and study their top-impression ads** [indie, 2022-03-01, trend-research-and-validation]: Keep roughly 10 direct competitors in a dashboard and pull their top-impression ads. Ask what they do that works, then adapt it within your own asset constraints rather than copying it. -- [GameMakers 2022-03 27:42](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1662) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Read falling ad CTR as a saturation signal** [indie, 2020-05-27, trend-research-and-validation]: One creator found tycoon interest had declined and click-through rate on tycoon ads was low. Treat falling or low CTR for a genre's ads as a warning and check genre saturation before investing. -- [TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**

## 5. Metrics, ROAS and stop rules (7)

- **Soft launch quietly and join players** [mixed, 2026-06-29, playtesting-and-feedback]: Release with a minimal announcement and light advertising, let a few players in, and fix stuck points in week one. Join servers on launch day. Watch ad traffic on day one for about 5 minutes and do not change settings yet. After a bad soft launch, step back and return with a fresh mind. -- [Roblox Creators 2021-11 5:33](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=333) [Roblox Creators 2021-07 22:48](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1368)
- **Use promo codes and trackable creator deals** [mixed, 2025-04-04, social-features-and-virality]: Give each YouTuber a unique in-game cash code to measure performance; pay small creators in items, ranks or skins; commission small YouTubers/TikTokers for a cheap springboard; tweets should match theme with link and incentive. -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:08:41](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4121) [Roblox 2018-07 8:16](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=496) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **For group-owned games, track ad revenue under the group** [indie, 2026-08-06, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: When the experience is group-owned, check revenue under the group rather than your personal account. Otherwise ad return will look like zero. -- [Raul 2026-08 2:44](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=164)
- **Adapt campaign mechanics when platform signals change instead of running unchanged setups** [indie, 2024-10-24, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: After iOS tracking changes (IDFA, 2023), advertisers who kept identical campaigns saw performance collapse. Do not give up on a channel entirely: iOS CPMs fell and competition thinned, so adapted setups could scale. A focused cross-functional team (engineering, data, UA) found workable tactics in a couple of weeks. Mobile-app context. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 12:52](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=772) [GameMakers 2023-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=122) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Announce status during dormancy instead of going silent** [indie, 2026-08-10, community-and-discord]: A game that goes quiet for a long stretch loses hype, and lurking Discord members don't return without new content or announcements. Post a status update even when the next release date is uncertain, and keep promoting the game between updates. -- [Cumin 2026-08 19:39](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1179) [Cumin 2026-08 21:41](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1301)
- **Use a 50-player gate to decide when to stop ads** [indie, 2026-08-02, discovery-algorithm]: Once Home recommendations appear, stop ads when the game stays above about 50 players after the ad period ends. This gives a concrete trigger alongside the time-based 7-14 day window. Self-reported, single creator. -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=490) [janisjanis01 2026-08 7:28](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=448)
- **Do not exclude paying players from rewarded ads** [indie, 2026-10-26, monetisation-other]: Deconstructor of Fun reports that claimed subscribers opted into rewarded ads at a higher rate than non-payers. Blocking paying users from ads may lose revenue, so keep rewarded ads open to them and measure opt-in rate by payer status. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 26:01](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=1561)

## 6. Off-platform: YouTube, TikTok, influencers, Discord, community (15)

- **Follow ad policy and disclosure rules** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Off-Roblox product promotion is independent advertising. Hide ads for users where PolicyService AreAdsAllowed is false (or kick if the whole game is an ad). Disclose clearly with standardised labels within 500 studs, localise disclosures, don't mimic Roblox's, no misleading content such as free Robux, no third-party ad servers. Seek lega… -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads)
- **Promoters, free-to-play switch, trade** [official, 2023-09-19, monetisation-other]: Pay YouTubers in group funds spent in your game; give creators early access or exclusives. When going free-to-play, give prior payers comparable perks. Make items (not currency) tradeable. Let players trade time for money; sell early unlock access not XP boosts. Label currency as in-game with fantasy names. -- [Roblox 2023-09 3:26](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=206) [Roblox Creators 2023-06 0:07](https://youtu.be/yn4V-fBdUm8?t=7)
- **Generate buzz with puzzles, teasers and leaks** [official, 2023-04-27, social-features-and-virality]: Cipher/treasure-hunt puzzles on Twitter, secret-account teasers, and accepting leaks as viral marketing; build a game hashtag and a stealth account before reveal; ask for community wishlists. -- [Roblox Creators 2023-04 0:46](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=46) [Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:49](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=169)
- **Use licensed IP or anime aesthetics where they fit** [mixed, 2026-10-28, game-ideas-and-genres]: Roblox promotes licensed IP (27 licenses, 500+ games greenlit). Sonic Speed Sim showed faithful mechanics make an IP game stand out. Anime maps to the 13-24 core. Check brand demographics match the genre. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing) [Roblox 2026-10 3:06](https://youtu.be/oxiaIhN5Ybo?t=186)
- **Use Share Links instead of deep links** [mixed, 2026-10-28, social-features-and-virality]: Share Links are the supported way to create public deep links and to track off-platform acquisition. Deep links are deprecated. Social links point to your channels, so use Share Links for trackable traffic. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/deeplinks)
- **Pace marketing and hype** [mixed, 2026-05-09, titles-descriptions]: Tease escalating over months, set release windows with buffer, cross-promote team posts, pitch brands with track record. -- [Roblox 2023-09 12:35](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=755) [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:14:44](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4484)
- **Prioritise a few first-party ad platforms over new networks and middlemen (mobile UA)** [indie, 2024-10-24, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: For mobile-app user acquisition, not Roblox-specific: master a few first-party channels (Google, Meta, TikTok, Snapchat, Apple Search) plus rewarded networks. Cut or limit DSP middlemen, whose reported 20-40% margin is hard to justify against using first-party algorithms directly. -- [GameMakers 2024-10 25:11](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1511) [GameMakers 2024-10 26:35](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1595) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Sell commissions and info products with a portfolio** [indie, 2025-07-06, business-and-payouts]: Take commissions for one specific skill and promote yourself on social media and dev Discords. Use the Talent Hub, DevForum and a simple portfolio site. Build many finished works first. SmartyRBX also suggests courses, tutoring and affiliate programs (30-50% commission claimed). His claim that a simple monetised game earns 10-20x commiss… -- [SmartyRBX 2025-06 15:00](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=900) [SmartyRBX 2025-06 17:39](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=1059) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Discount income claims from course sellers** [indie, 2025-12-14, business-and-payouts]: A UGC creator noted that many people promoting UGC earn from selling courses, not from UGC items. Ask for item sales data and separate course revenue from marketplace revenue before treating an income claim as a benchmark. -- [Britt Drake 2025-12 9:45](https://youtu.be/ncZn8_hopuc?t=585)
- **Don't model revenue on myths: 1 Robux per visit, paid UGC ads, bloated shops or AFK farming** [indie, 2026-10-28, common-mistakes]: The 1-Robux-per-visit claim is false; one game earned far more per visit from purchases. 200 Robux per item across 17 paid UGC items produced no measurable lift next day. Cut shop items players won't buy. Avoid AFK-farm or pure gambling designs chasing Premium payouts, which are risky for player quality. -- [Erthlord 2026-07 9:32](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=572) [Perstile 2025-05 0:40](https://youtu.be/ET3fRqaFmFc?t=40)
- **Post devlogs consistently for about two months before launch** [indie, 2026-05-03, community-and-discord]: Pick one platform (TikTok, YouTube or Discord) and post dev logs on a steady cadence for about 2 months before release. One example built 5,000 followers in that window. Pre-launch hype was a feature of popular games. -- [rasdev 2026-05 8:23](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=503) [janisjanis01 2024-06 5:47](https://youtu.be/NWyK_PbFdxE?t=347)
- **Host a community wiki that members can edit** [indie, 2025-11-23, community-and-discord]: A community wiki gives new players one hub to catch up. If you need a wiki, host it on wiki.gg rather than Fandom, which has heavy intrusive ads (2025 source). -- [Cumin 2025-11 3:38](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=218) [Last Level Studios 2019-09 8:45](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=525)
- **Source ideas from real life, social media and non-Roblox media** [indie, 2026-10-23, game-ideas-and-genres]: Use recess games, things people love or hate, popular shows, memes, TikTok trends with no Roblox version, and books or movies. Clone makers only look at Roblox. Keep an inspiration bank and play many games with friends. -- [rasdev 2026-08 2:51](https://youtu.be/17DGro3FUlM?t=171) [rasdev 2026-08 3:19](https://youtu.be/17DGro3FUlM?t=199)
- **Keep progression codes on-platform** [indie, 2025-11-23, monetisation-other]: Don't gate progression behind codes posted on off-platform Discord or Twitter. Codes expire, and the gate pushes players off-platform before they follow on-platform. Keep reward gates inside the experience. -- [Cumin 2025-11 5:20](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=320)
- **Prioritize quality over raw volume** [indie, 2025-12-22, trailers-and-video-marketing]: janisjanis01 shifted to fewer, better-edited Shorts after feedback; #7Phantom says invest in genuinely good ads. Keep expectations realistic: most Shorts spike then die, and a single flop isn't a verdict. -- [#7Phantom 2025-12 0:41](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=41) [janisjanis01 2025-06 8:39](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=519)

## 7. Other (23)

- **Poll campaign status after creation; new campaigns start IN_REVIEW** [official, 2026-10-28, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Campaigns stay IN_REVIEW until ad-policy review completes. Poll campaigns:batchGetStatus (up to 100 IDs per call) until SERVING, NOT_SERVING or REJECTED. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- **Groups: moderate actively and do not promote violative content** [official, 2026-10-28, age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]: As group owner, make a reasonable effort to address harmful behavior and proactively moderate using group audit logs. Do not use the group to promote or upload violative content, since this can lead to enforcement on your account and permanent group deletion. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/group-guidelines)
- **Rewarded ads: deterministic, account-bound, no RNG** [official, 2026-10-28, common-mistakes]: Under 2026 standards state the exact reward ('Watch to receive [Specific Item]'); no 'Random Legendary Item'. Tickets for a later draw are allowed. Rewards must be developer products, not Robux. Tradable ad currency enables alt farming. Don't let ads hurt the character. Not allowed in free-form creation or AI-interaction experiences. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads)
- **Don't over-hype or over-promote early** [official, 2023-04-27, common-mistakes]: Over-promoting 6-8 months out loses interest and raises expectations; overhyping makes an MVP-then-iterate release harder. -- [Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:07](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=127) [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:15:27](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4527)
- **Configure purchases and ads after publishing** [official, 2026-10-28, monetisation-gamepasses]: Set up in-game purchases and immersive ads from the Creator Dashboard monetization features once the game is published. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience)
- **Set rewarded-ad reward value carefully** [official, 2026-10-28, pricing]: Around 3-10 Robux equivalent so players don't abandon mid-ad; 30 seconds of watching must feel worth it without cannibalising purchases. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon) [Roblox Creators 2025-08 17:45](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1065)
- **Use prompts that need 1–2 clicks with a clear CTA** [official, 2026-10-28, thumbnails-icons]: Rewarded or ad prompts should need only one or two clicks and state a concrete action, for example 'Watch this ad for 2x the coins!'. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads)
- **Theme each release** [official, 2023-08-18, updates-live-ops]: A constrained theme (e.g. domestic, fantasy, polar pets) makes ideation easier, marketing simpler and avoids retreading ground. Tie it to other systems (furniture, cosmetics) and cultural references. -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 15:37](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=937) [Roblox Creators 2021-10 5:18](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=318)
- **Join platform events and coordinate with IP holders** [official, 2026-10-12, updates-live-ops]: Join cross-promotional and platform events when spare time exists, not during update crunch. Roblox platform live ops is quarterly and moving toward more frequent events. Time-limited licenses can power events. With an IP holder, plan a roadmap, sync weekly and have the first post-launch updates planned. -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:03](https://youtu.be/oxiaIhN5Ybo?t=783) [Roblox 2025-04 52:34](https://youtu.be/vTc2wciWukE?t=3154)
- **Rotate shop stock and refresh rewarded ads** [official, 2026-10-28, updates-live-ops]: Rotate shop stock and introduce new items regularly so the shop feels fresh. Refresh the rewarded ad setup with new reward items to keep interest. -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/monetization-foundations) [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon)
- **Use time-limited IP licenses for crossovers** [devforum, 2026-06-24, events-and-seasonal-content]: Time-limited licenses suit live ops, promotional drops and limited crossovers without long-term commitment. -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4701733)
- **Expect low-quality cold traffic and drop-off after ads end** [indie, 2026-09-08, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Ads optimise for the cheapest player, so lobbies can look bot-like and early stats low. Player spikes fall back after ads end (kra_DeZ: 40-80 during ads, 5-10 after). Don't panic or restart ads repeatedly; Roblox uses a rolling 7-day window. -- [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:08](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=668) [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:49](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=709)
- **Do not advertise a buggy or unstable build** [indie, 2026-07-08, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Duckable spent 32,000 Robux on a game that broke, leaving the balance negative. Ads cannot fix a game problem and players will still leave. Stabilise and retain first. -- [rasdev 2026-07 2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163) [Duckable 2026-05 23:35](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1415)
- **Judge ad impact on repeated exposure, not last touch** [indie, 2026-10-26, ads-and-sponsored-experiences]: Deconstructor of Fun cites roughly eight impressions before a player takes an action. Single-click attribution will undercount ads that build awareness across multiple touches. Compare cohorts over a window rather than crediting the final click. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 41:14](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2474)
- **Vet sponsor partners carefully** [indie, 2022-08-26, brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]: Sponsored artist concerts were widely disliked and poorly rated, while some sponsor events (Nazix, 21 Pilots) were well received. Quality and fit of the partner matter. This is a low-advice anecdote. -- [ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 27:15](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=1635) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Do not announce or leak content you may not ship** [indie, 2022-08-26, events-and-seasonal-content]: Cancelled sponsored events and scrapped near-finished events frustrated players. -- [ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 22:26](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=1346) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Consider separating PvP from PvE** [indie, 2025-07-31, genre-mechanics]: So PvP players don't block campaign progress. -- [Cumin 2025-07 15:21](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=921)
- **Make sure each pass grants the item it advertises** [indie, 2026-09-27, monetisation-gamepasses]: A pass that shows as owned but gives no item causes refunds and complaints. Verify every pass's grant path against its listed benefit before launch, and rewrite a broken grant script from scratch rather than patching it. -- [janisjanis01 2026-09 8:45](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=525) [janisjanis01 2023-09 6:50](https://youtu.be/9aQpeTCZ7yM?t=410)
- **Make solo play viable or clearly label it unsupported** [indie, 2026-06-24, player-psychology-and-game-feel]: If the game advertises 3-4 players but solo players cannot find a party, they quit. Either make solo play genuinely fun or state clearly that it is unsupported. This is recent advice (June 2026). -- [Cumin 2026-06 16:53](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1013)
- **Raise prices temporarily during traffic surges, then revert** [indie, 2026-09-01, pricing]: During a major promotion or push, raise gamepass and developer product prices to capture high-intent demand. Revert to normal prices once traffic settles so long-term conversion and player trust are preserved. -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 5:26](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=326)
- **Make clips come from the core loop, not ad slots** [indie, 2025-09-16, social-features-and-virality]: A shareable moment only helps if it is part of the core loop. A clip must deliver value to players, not just act as an ad slot for the game. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 17:46](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=1066) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Write a complete investor application** [indie, 2020-04-01, studio-business-funding-and-publishing]: Include your role, game details, what funds are for (art, hiring, ads), the amount needed, and a timeline to completion. -- [TheDevKing 2020-04 8:09](https://youtu.be/VOKFE8Qi16c?t=489) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Weigh a publisher against self-publishing** [indie, 2026-10-22, studio-business-funding-and-publishing]: Self-publishing means doing both development and distribution (marketing, user acquisition, store presence). The creator argues few studios do both well, so consider a publisher when you lack distribution skill or capacity. Self-reported, low-advice source; check publisher terms and revenue split before signing. -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 45:23](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2723)

## If-then rules for ads

- For ad creatives, propose a matrix over duration and aspect ratio rather than a few hand-picked variants. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- If the user asks whether to run ads on a new game, first check retention and the first-time experience; if weak, advise fixing them before spending. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If advising a first-time budget, suggest about $10/day for ~10 days (range $5-20/day), Maximize Plays, All Players, all regions and devices; label dollar figures as self-reported indie claims. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user asks when to stop, say stop once home recommendation impressions exceed sponsored impressions in Acquisition analytics, and don't restart repeatedly (rolling 7-day window). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user proposes a 1-2 day campaign, cite Roblox's guidance of at least 1-2 weeks, with 3-5 days minimum before judging results. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If CTR is below 1%, replace the creative; target 2.5-3%+. Run thumbnail A/B tests before large spend. For daily vs lifetime, recommend daily (official + CallMeDogey) and note waveshine and kra_DeZ use lifetime. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the game is old or lapsed after a major update, recommend Maximize Plays + Reactivate Users for 1-2 weeks; check the 20,000 lapsed / 10,000 recent minimums. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If asked about search ads, advise relevant high-volume keywords, max 10 per Ad Set, single-keyword Ad Sets for comparison, and no audience targeting. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user wants rewarded video, check eligibility (13+, ID, 2FA, public unrestricted game, 2,000 monthly visitors, maturity questionnaire), call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync just before display, show clear non-random non-Robux rewards, and track opt-in (13-15%+) and frequency (2.5-3+). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If ads are placed in an experience, check AreAdsAllowed per player, add disclosure labels, register campaigns 5+ days ahead and obey billboard size limits; for brand deals recommend in-game integrations over standalone games plus legal review. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If indie claims conflict with official docs on mechanics, prefer official docs and the most recent date, and tell the user to check the live Ads Manager UI since settings change. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user asks to start or fund a sponsored campaign, first confirm eligibility via campaign-options and check that the game holds players and has no stability problems. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a campaign shows high impressions but low plays or CTR near 0.3%, recommend pausing and fixing the game before spending more. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a campaign is performing, recommend scaling in small tranches and measuring each before the next. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- When recommending budgets, state the unit (Robux, ad credits or USD) and the genre and explain the range. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Use day-7 retention and sessions as the success metric, not impressions or clicks. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- When writing API code, include an idempotency key, poll status, use durationInDays and batch status checks, and use PAUSED rather than CANCELLED for temporary stops. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Prefer the Oct 2026 official docs and the 250 Kids/Select threshold over older figures; mark indie numbers as self-reported. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a user is planning a large spend, recommend tranches and a return calculation (Robux returned versus spent) before committing. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If an experience is 17+ rated, don't plan ad-driven acquisition without first checking current Roblox rules. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: since 2025-01-31 Roblox staff allow Sponsored Experiences and Search Ads for restricted (17+) and paid-access experiences, but 17+ experiences cannot serve ads to users aged 13-17. Ads Manager access needs age 13+ and a verified email (no ID check for advertisers); ad PUBLISHERS (monetising with ads) need 18+ and ID verification (DevForum 'Advertising paid access & restricted (17+) experiences' 2025-01-31; Creator Docs ads-manager fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- If asked about cost, convert all credits to Robux at 285 per credit and state the Robux cost per play. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a user is advertising a marketplace item, use Sponsored Items (min 10 Robux/day, 24h-28 day cycle); otherwise use Ads Manager or the Advertising API. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If home-recommendation players exceed ad-driven players, recommend reducing or stopping paid credits. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Before recommending spend, confirm the core loop and retention are working; if not, advise fixing them first. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- When a user needs to change a thumbnail mid-campaign, say the active ad must be cancelled and restarted (one creator; verify). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Compute refund for early cancel as full days remaining only, not partial days. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Treat indie numbers as self-reported and flag low-advice items; prefer official 2026 docs over 2020-2025 creator posts. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Flag unit mismatches between sources (credits vs Robux vs USD) instead of mixing them. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Pre-roll video ads need no code; don't write custom ad code for them. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the game has no working monetisation or weak day-1 retention, advise against paid spend until those are fixed. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a user asks for a budget, give test and scale ranges separately (e.g. ~100 Robux test vs 10k-100k scale-up), noting that creators disagree. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Before recommending an objective, ask whether the goal is testing or sustained growth; 2026 creators favour engagement objectives. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Always recommend a custom daily budget and disabling auto-reload. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If an ad shows zero impressions after ~30 minutes, check campaign status and eligibility before concluding it failed. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Advise against pulling and reposting campaigns; judge results after the learning period and at least 1-2 weeks, unless thresholds clearly fail. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If CTR is high but day-1 retention or plays are low, diagnose audience and messaging mismatch first. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Convert credits to Robux using the actual purchase price (about 250-285 Robux each), not a single assumed rate. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Treat indie ROI claims as self-reported; do not present them as official Roblox figures. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- For European or under-13 audiences, check the 13+ and verification requirements before planning. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a campaign's audience is allowed to be 18+ and the goal is retention or monetization, test an O18 segment against all players before scaling. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a traffic or CCU spike coincides with an event or admin action, do not credit it to ads until the event is ruled out. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- When citing rewarded-video revenue or sponsored-ad prevalence, use the official Sep 2026 IR figures and note that the ad-prevalence figure is correlational. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If asked about co-marketing or UA funding from Roblox, cite T17596 and label it as indie-reported, not official. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If asked about pre-roll reach or revenue, state that Roblox Plus subscribers won't see pre-roll ads (T17663). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If asked about the size of Roblox brand activations, cite N7475 with its Q4 2025 date. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Do not treat the 2026-09 pre-roll 'coming soon' status as launched without checking current official docs. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a campaign targets lapsed players, then report the share of players who return within 7 days alongside cost per activation. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If cost per activation is above Robux return, then pause the campaign rather than scaling. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a player appears after several ad impressions but no click, then credit the ad campaign on a multi-day window, not on the last click. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If running Discord quest buys for an existing game, then split reporting between new users and lapsed reengagement. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a publisher runs several games, then test audience and avatar combinations per product before pooling budget. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a creator activation drives a download spike, then do not attribute that spike to paid ads without a separate test. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If CTR is high but plays are low, then review the landing experience and the ad promise before increasing budget. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a creative's CTR is below your kill threshold, stop it. Compare IPM and watch time before scaling any survivor. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If you need a new creative pipeline, plan 20-50 concepts per cycle, sized to monthly spend, and build them from a persona-by-trend matrix. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If you want to test a challenger against a champion, launch it in its own campaign; never add it to the champion's campaign. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If you plan to enter a new country, run a small paid campaign there first and scale only when retention, CPI or ROI look good. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If CPI exceeds the LTV you forecast, pause scaling and fix retention or monetisation before adding spend. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If ad-sourced players churn at high rates per 1,000 impressions (100 per 1,000 is very bad), treat the creative or audience as a quality problem, not a volume problem. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the influencer channel is TikTok and you need trackable installs, add Spark Ads cost (about double spend) to your budget or choose another channel. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If you are funding influencers for the first time, set expectations that the first campaigns are for learning and that cutting spend later increases install decline. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the first 8-credit-per-day, 2-day campaign shows traction, step to 16 credits per day for 2 days; do not jump straight to full budget. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Use ad credits, not Robux, when budgeting 2026 campaigns; treat 2020-22 Robux and Facebook numbers as historical. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a campaign's CTR is healthy but QPTR is under ~1%, pause budget increases and diagnose the first session before scaling. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user asks whether ads can fix a weak or unoptimised game, advise against it; ads should only seed initial players. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If a launch-day bug is still open, delay campaigns until the build is stable. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Before launch, check that no unintended campaigns are running concurrently. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Treat mobile-app UA items (DSP, mediation, iOS IDFA) as off-Roblox; apply them only when the user asks about non-Roblox apps. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- Quote the source (indie, self-reported) when citing janisjanis, GameMakers or rasdev figures. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- If the user asks to advertise an external link, point them to Social Links and the description, and warn that most external services are 13+. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- If the user plans a kids-targeted launch, factor in the reduced advertising reach from age gating. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- If running ads, then wait 24h learning and 48h delay, use Ads Manager for ROI at day 30, and check Creator Analytics for retention harm; for rewarded video check impressions, opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+). [analytics-metrics]
- If a metric spiked after an ad push or update, wait about 7 days and compare the settled baseline before calling the change a win or failure. [analytics-metrics]
- If an ad campaign is under 48 hours old, do not decide anything; plays can be credited for up to 30 days. [analytics-metrics]
- If ad CTR or cost per play is weak and the overview stats are below the 50th percentile, fix the game before raising spend. [analytics-metrics]
- When an ad campaign produces no Home Recommendations after its run, recommend stopping ads and pivoting rather than buying more. [analytics-metrics]
- When playtime during ads is under 10 minutes, warn that the game is unlikely to reach the algorithm; under 20 minutes on the algorithm is a weak sign. [analytics-metrics]
- If computing ad ROI, convert gross Robux to net first (×0.7 or current DevEx rate), then compare with ad spend in USD. [analytics-metrics]
- If a paid campaign is under 7 days old, do not judge D7 retention; use 7-day moving averages and say the window is incomplete. [analytics-metrics]
- If new-player data shows low engagement after ads, split new versus returning players before cutting features. [analytics-metrics]
- If evaluating an influencer or creator campaign, use a minimum 7-day click window and never compare it with UA on a 7-30 day window. [analytics-metrics]
- If a campaign is giveaway-based, assume lower organic uplift (~30%) than non-giveaway posts (up to ~100% of attributed traffic). [analytics-metrics]
- Keep any brand character tied to the game's existing story and audience. Do not use it as a standalone ad that players would see as off-topic. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- If a user asks when money arrives, give the relevant hold: 60 days for earned Robux, 30 days Creator Store, up to 7 days paid access, ads on the 25th of next month. [business-and-payouts]
- If a user is offered a percentage deal for advertising, advise against it; if a studio buyout is offered, compare a 20-50% cut with keeping the game. [business-and-payouts]
- If a user asks about ad or game profitability, first multiply gross Robux by 0.7, then add creator rewards, then subtract publishing fees and ad spend. [business-and-payouts]
- If a user asks about valuations or buyouts, compare the offer to recoupable revenue and avoid giving equity for advertising. [business-and-payouts]
- For UGC or avatar item plans, assume a first-year loss unless the user has tested demand; include upload, publish and promotion costs. [business-and-payouts]
- Do not cite promotional quotes (Q1757, Q1758) as evidence of payout quality or ease of use. [business-and-payouts]
- If state must live outside components, consider Charm atoms with replication instead of ad hoc remotes. [client-server-and-remotes]
- If a user wants to buy ads for a game with poor retention, then advise fixing the loop first. [common-mistakes]
- If designing rewarded ads, then make rewards deterministic, account-bound, developer products, with no RNG. [common-mistakes]
- If the user plans ad spend, first confirm bugs are fixed and check CTR on a small test before scaling. [common-mistakes]
- If a user wants to spend on ads or sponsorship, first check retention, bugs and the economy; advise against spend until they pass. [common-mistakes]
- If a new item restates an existing rule about ads or marketing rescuing weak games, skip it rather than duplicating the rule. [common-mistakes]
- If asked about influencer outreach, suggest business email for larger creators and small creators (1-10k subs) for free playthroughs. [community-and-discord]
- If planning pre-launch promotion, consider multi-platform audience building but keep one primary Discord hub. [community-and-discord]
- When advising on promotion sequencing, recommend community setup before paid acquisition, and paid acquisition before social SDK or commerce features. [community-and-discord]
- If new events are created, keep them private until promotion time; if content is flagged as too mature, modify it with PolicyService and resubmit. [development-process-and-speed]
- If the user is considering paid user acquisition or a funding raise, ask for traction evidence first (T18574). [development-process-and-speed]
- If bounce rate is high, align the icon with the first 30-180 seconds and fix onboarding before spending on ads. [discovery-algorithm]
- If using ads, spread small spend over 2-4 weeks, watch for Home impressions, and stop when organic sustains the game. [discovery-algorithm]
- If no Home recs after 7-14 days of ads, recommend reassessing or moving on, and label it as indie heuristic. [discovery-algorithm]
- If the 7-14 day ad decision rule conflicts with the 28-day window, follow the 28-day window and say so. [discovery-algorithm]
- If a game shows Home recommendations in the acquisition tab, treat it as having entered the algorithm and apply the stop-ads check. [discovery-algorithm]
- If the game holds above about 50 players after ads end, recommend stopping ads; otherwise keep the 7-14 day window before deciding. [discovery-algorithm]
- If an update is major, then align it with a seasonal moment and promote a sign-up pad beforehand. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- If planning ads, first confirm onboarding funnel, bugs and age-rating settings. [first-session-onboarding]
- If a mobile ad game is the inspiration, search the Roblox catalog for existing remakes first and skip it if one is already widely copied. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- If asked how to get YouTubers to play a game, then recommend a first message with screenshots, a trailer, a direct playable link and a concrete perk. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- If evaluating a campaign, then compare organic installs before, during and after, and look at 365-day value, not only day 30. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- If the game is weak or unreleased, then prioritize game quality and pre-launch hype over Discord or TikTok building; treat indie numbers as unverified. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- If reporting influencer performance, include CPI, store ranking, DAU and retention alongside direct installs. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- If expanding to a new country, then run a paid UA test before always-on campaigns. [localization-and-international]
- If a design sets a large group size (e.g. 15-player campaigns), then size the queue, wait-time and fallback behaviour for that threshold; do not reuse 4-player PvE assumptions (N6407). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- For items tagged low-advice or indie self-reported, record them as single-mention and do not promote them to multiple-creators strength without corroboration. [mindset-and-strategy]
- If an ad creative underperforms, stop tweaking it and shift budget to the winners. [mindset-and-strategy]
- If judging early revenue, separate sponsored traffic from organic before drawing conclusions (N8111). [monetisation-devproducts]
- If the user runs or plans ads, check that at least a few passes are live first. [monetisation-gamepasses]
- Do not recommend paid ads or traffic until organic conversion is proven. [monetisation-other]
- Never promote free-Robux offers or secret methods; treat them as scams. [monetisation-other]
- If suggesting codes for progression, then keep them on-platform; do not gate on off-platform-only codes. [monetisation-other]
- If recommending an affiliate program or off-platform paid tier, then label it as unverified and check policy first. [monetisation-other]
- If a user is planning paid UA, warn about summer 2026 competition and recommend validating monetisation organically first. [monetisation-other]
- If you run player-initiated rewarded ads, do not exclude paying or subscribed players from them by default (T17967), and track opt-in rates by payer status. [monetisation-other]
- Exclude off-platform AAA revenue claims (N8154 to N8158, N8167) from Roblox monetisation advice; they do not apply to Roblox. [monetisation-other]
- If the game is advertised for multiple players, verify solo matchmaking works or state clearly that it is unsupported. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- If the user is running paid ads, advise joining their own server to see where players leave, and shipping fixes between ad days. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- If a user asks who should test onboarding, suggest non-developers such as marketing staff, and have them log funnel friction and confirm tracking events fire. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- If a traffic spike or big promotion is planned, raise prices temporarily and set a reversion date. [pricing]
- If asked about ad budgets, recommend a small test spend before a full campaign and cite the 8,000-Robux no-testing example as the cautionary case. [pricing]
- If asked for current prices (ad credits, DevEx, gamepass norms), prefer newer sources and tell the user figures may be outdated. [pricing]
- If a user asks how to fund publishing, testing or ads with Robux, cite the wallet repurchase rate of $0.0038/Robux (N7522) and note tax applies. [pricing]
- If ads bring players who leave fast, then fix the game, not the ad; use Beta mode to optimise out of home recs. [retention-d1-d7]
- If a source describes a mobile-OS or ad-tracking change (such as IDFA) rather than a Roblox platform change, file it as context only and do not add it to Roblox platform rules. [roblox-platform-changes]
- Label indie UA or acquisition-cost figures as self-reported and unverified unless an OFFICIAL source confirms them. [roblox-platform-changes]
- If a rewarded ad or Robux transfer is involved, store the returned IDs and correlate them with ProcessReceipt. [scripting-and-performance]
- If planning promotion with little budget, then pitch small creators with a playable build and unique promo codes; do not pay big influencers early. [social-features-and-virality]
- If planning TikTok content, then post clippable funny moments, not plain 'play my game' posts; fix bugs first. [social-features-and-virality]
- If TikTok traffic claims conflict, prefer the 2026-10 rasdev figure but flag it as self-reported and mention the 2026-01 janisjanis fade. [social-features-and-virality]
- If a devlog underperforms on TikTok, test Instagram Reels with the same clip before deciding the platform is wrong. [social-features-and-virality]
- If a game has empty servers for solo players, prioritize fixing that before pushing friend-invite campaigns. [social-features-and-virality]
- If the user has no ad budget, prioritize TikTok and YouTube Shorts clips over paid promotion (T18712, Q1928). [social-features-and-virality]
- If a user asks about player-made promotion or contests, suggest a contest with Discord rules and a Robux prize as one option, labeled as a single indie example. [social-features-and-virality]
- If asked about Roblox program support, describe it as mentorship, promotion, and possible future incentives, and flag that this is indie-reported (self-reported) rather than official. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- If a QA program needs testers, decide structure first, then advertise publicly and scale pay by revenue and bug volume. [team-and-budget]
- If choosing leaders, select by personality, maturity and trustworthiness and publish a promotion path. [team-and-budget]
- If a project has not yet passed soft-launch validation, do not hire a full marketing team; hire at most one internal marketer or use a trusted partner. [team-and-budget]
- If a user asks whether to launch ads or promote a game, first confirm a full-game multiplayer playtest pass has been completed. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- If a user's game has low CTR/PTR vs genre peers, then redesign thumbnails first and run multiple variants in an ad test. [thumbnails-icons]
- If asked how many thumbnails, then give 3–5 for personalization and 10+ for ad tests, noting current Ads Manager caps should be verified. [thumbnails-icons]
- If testing, then change one element, equal budget and duration, and promote the winner. [thumbnails-icons]
- If the footage contains voiceover, narration, lyric music, promo text, cinematic trailer styling or over-enhanced visuals, rebuild the video from real in-game capture. [thumbnails-icons]
- If a campaign creative underperforms its peers, disable it and test a replacement; keep the campaign at up to 10 creatives. [thumbnails-icons]
- If the icon fails the half-second glance or one-sentence test, redesign it before spending on ads. [thumbnails-icons]
- If CTR is high but players or PTR are low, fix the game and retention before scaling ads. [thumbnails-icons]
- For sponsored ads, produce a 16:9 thumbnail as well as the square icon. [thumbnails-icons]
- If the user asks how to test thumbnails or icons, prepare two thumbnails plus an icon and run about $20 per variant as ad tests. [thumbnails-icons]
- If the user wants to change a thumbnail on a running ad, explain it cannot be swapped and recommend a new ad creative. [thumbnails-icons]
- If sponsored-tab CTR is weak, test a stronger icon first, then 16:9 variants, and compare results. [thumbnails-icons]
- If ad CTR is below about 1.5%, check targeting and campaign settings before replacing the thumbnail. [thumbnails-icons]
- If citing RoyalDev figures or tool claims, label them self-reported and low-advice (promotional demos). [thumbnails-icons]
- If CTR is high but retention or PTR is weak, do not recommend scaling the ad spend (existing rule). [thumbnails-icons]
- If a thumbnail or icon test is planned, compare against the existing ad creative baseline and record both CTRs. [thumbnails-icons]
- If a developer is publishing an update, suggest adding promotional text alongside the existing freshness tag (T17623). [titles-descriptions]
- Do not treat promotional text as a replacement for keeping the title consistent; the title should stay unchanged across updates. [titles-descriptions]
- If the audience (kids, teens, etc.) is undefined, define it before writing the title or marketing posts. [titles-descriptions]
- If asked to make a trailer for the Roblox game page, then use only real current gameplay, no voiceover, narration, promo text, lyrics music or cinematic scenes. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If making an external trailer, then use 60-90s, a 5-10s action hook, features in 15-20s, title card with CTA, and on-beat music. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If the game is weak or unfinished, then advise improving the game before heavy trailer investment, though devlogs and rough clips are fine for early awareness. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If planning social content, then recommend TikTok/Shorts, a first-seconds hook, and a consistent cadence; track videos over 1,000 views and repeat the common element. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If measuring creator or video campaigns, then use at least a 7-day click window. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If the game has a notable update, then submit a Today's Picks nomination with a trailer and core-mechanic footage. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If the task is paid Meta or Google ad creative, then use polished cinematics and looping motion graphics rather than lo-fi footage (T17951). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If the task is TikTok-style organic short form, then favor lo-fi developer diaries and organic storytelling over polished ad styling (T17951, Q1839). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- If a keyword search shows 0-2 games, treat the niche as open but verify off-platform demand. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If a trend's origin is TikTok or a meme, verify it is still rising and ship fast, or skip. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If the user wants a launch plan, include an initial traffic source (ads or short-form content) since no organic lift exists at zero visits. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If a genre's ad trend has been observed for less than 90 days, collect more data before acting. [trend-research-and-validation]
