- Treat impressions per ad as a quality proxy, not a direct demand measure. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If genre ad CTR is low or falling, lower the priority of that genre and check saturation. [trend-research-and-validation]
- Do not apply mobile UA market figures to Roblox decisions without Roblox-specific data. [trend-research-and-validation]
- Save ads from a clean browsing setup so the research reflects competitor creatives, not your own ad-feed bias. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If ad CTR for a creative is near 2%, test a replacement creative aiming for ~3% before scaling spend. [trend-research-and-validation]
- If the task is paid promotion of UGC, first ask whether the content is commissioned from creators (T18661), since paid amplification applies to that case and not to creator-channel influencer posts. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- If submitting for Off-Platform Featuring, submit at least 7 days ahead and do not edit after submission. [updates-live-ops]
- If the team is about to run ads, confirm fresh content, icon and thumbnail are ready first. [updates-live-ops]

## Where sources disagree

- **How quickly to act on results** (ab-testing-and-experiments): Roblox DevForum: do not launch from the first 24 hours, wait for full duration. | Roblox Creators: iterate frequently on small changes and judge by retention while spending on ads (different context, not a controlled experiment). | Deconstructor of Fun: judge creatives over more than a week or two.
- **Daily vs lifetime budget** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): CallMeDogey: always daily, never lifetime (steady flow signals consistency). | Roblox Creators (2025-06): daily budget with recommendations over lifetime. | waveshine (2024): lifetime budget gives waves of players and beats daily. | kra_DeZ (2026): lifetime budget in ad credits for thumbnail tests and main runs. **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Whether ad traffic drives the algorithm** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): MoseyTube, Tizzy, Alex Builds Games: ads give enough data for home recs. | Roblox official: ads are independent of RFY ranking; their users are excluded from RFY signals; ads add incremental players. | ItsZyonic (2026-09): ads alone didn't catch the algorithm; organic metrics climbed after he turned ads off, though he also said CCU dropped when ads went off. | Tizzy: sponsored traffic doesn't pus…
- **Importance of ads vs other promotion** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): DevForum 2024: ads preferred over social/YouTuber outreach, whose spikes fade. | ItsZyonic 2023: avoid relying on ads, use sponsors; SmartyRBX and TheDevKing recommend creator sponsorships. | CallMeDogey 2026 and AlvinBlox: ads are a minor step, use sparingly. | DitchyDevelopments: ads are the fastest way to get players.
- **Campaign length and duration** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Roblox (2025-06): at least 1-2 weeks. | waveshine (2024): one-day ads. | kra_DeZ: 3 day test and 4-5 day main. | Tizzy: at least 3 days, ideally a week. | MoseyTube: ~10 days. | Alex: 7 days. | Older short-run advice is outdated by the official guidance. **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- **Best time to start ads** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): MoseyTube: at launch, since Roblox needs data. | Tizzy 2026-07: about two weeks after launch. | Roblox: start small, fix UX from first players, then scale. | DitchyDevelopments and Tizzy: only with solid retention.
- **Typical budget size and units** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Erthlord (citing Bubix): about 50 ad credits per day as standard. | Yann: $15/day for 7-10 days for most genres; $50-100 total for a beginner. | Duckable: $16/day for 10 days, and says a huge budget is not needed. | bubDEV: lifetime 150-400 Robux-equivalent over 5-7 days. | Units differ (credits, Robux, USD), so figures are not directly comparable.
- **Whether to spend on ads before the game retains players** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): rasdev (Apr 2026): seed the algorithm with small Robux ad spend early. | rasdev (Oct 2026): only pay for ads once the game already holds players. | Duckable (May 2026): spend only after the game retains players. | Newer rasdev advice (Oct 2026) supersedes the April seeding advice.
- **Whether ads affect ranking** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): rasdev (Oct 2026): ads raise player counts but not ranking. | janisjanis (Sep 2025, Feb 2026): a campaign pushed the game into the algorithm. | Treat the algorithm-entry claim as self-reported and unverified.
- **Speed of results** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): janisjanis: a scaled campaign produced a quick surge in players. | tef (Oct 2026): expect a slow start while the algorithm learns the audience.
- **Ads versus creators or organic** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): janisjanis (Dec 2024): creator videos are the best way to gain players. | janisjanis (Jan 2026): paid ads likely beat organic TikTok unless videos reach millions of views.
- **Targeting narrowness** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): bubDEV and Yann: keep all devices and regions to maximise reach. | janisjanis (Dec 2024): targeted US and Canada, mobile and PC, which produced the best ad.
- **Whether off-platform video (YouTube Shorts, TikTok) can replace paid Roblox ads** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Matt The Method: free YouTube Shorts drove the game's earnings with no ad spend (T13174). | Raul: free Shorts drove over 1,000 views but barely moved visits (max about 20 players); paid reach was needed (T13662). | VuukStudios: prefer Roblox ads over TikTok/Shorts for early traffic (T13822).
- **Whether thumbnails can be changed while an ad runs** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): VuukStudios: swap thumbnails while ads run when CTR is below 1% (T13027). | Raul: Roblox does not allow a thumbnail swap on an active ad; cancel and restart (T13657).
- **Whether Roblox ads pay back** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Positive: Erthlord's campaign lifted players from 23 to 140 (T13150); Arow saw revenue jump after ads (T12759); VuukStudios says Roblox ads is king and a good game gets 10k players for 5k Robux (Q1438, Q1400). | Negative: Quez lost 4,964 Robux net (N4830); MonarchDev lost about $200 across two games (T13715); PineappleDev lost on 4,000 Robux (T12762); JustDucksey's ad players barely converted (T1…
- **Whether ads are needed at all for a good game** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): GnomeCode: focus on making a good game; the algorithm will pick it up (Q1280). | VuukStudios: the algorithm cannot pick you up if your game just sits (Q1421).
- **Campaign length** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): VuukStudios and existing guidance: 1-2 weeks (N5452, T13835). | Raul and Arow: 7 days (T13658, T13401). | Short bursts of 2-3 days (BloxxedDev N4642, Quez N4822, PineappleDev N4589) produced visits but weak retention.
- **Audience targeting breadth** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Arow: All Players for acquisition, Recent Players for win-back (T13400). | Quez: narrowed to Indonesia and Malaysia, action genre (N4823). | Roblox Creator Docs: sponsored items require a target audience (T14835).
- **Bid level effect on placement** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): JustDucksey: low bids (0.01 per play) push ads to less prominent placements (T12574). | Roblox Creator Docs: display share is proportional to daily budget relative to rival bidders; doubling a bid roughly doubles impressions versus a rival (T14834). | Note: mechanisms differ between experience ads and sponsored items.
- **Campaign objective** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing rule: set the goal to Maximize Plays. | JustVeryYellow: Maximize place visits (T16331). | LovJak: engagement objective targeting all players (T16636, T16544). | RoyalDev: engagement or audience targeting; budget and start time barely matter (T16593).
- **Breadth of targeting** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing rule: broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players. | BubuTheDev: narrow to US and Canada, then to the spending age and gender (T16143, T16144). | Lnor: narrowing to verified 18-24 PC and mobile raised CTR from ~1.5% to 8%+ (T16534).
- **When to judge or kill an ad** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing rule: kill weak ads early using CTR and play-rate thresholds. | RoyalDev: do not judge after 2-3 days of on/off cycling; that produced the worst results (T16595).
- **Audience targeting approach** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Alex Builds Games: vary audience and avatar per product in a media-buying style to find winners (T17905). | Existing knowledge file rule: use broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players. Source not named in this batch.
- **Whether the second campaign improved on the first** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): MonarchDev: second campaign had higher CTR than the first, with no exact figures (T17853, N7632). Cited as evidence for testing creatives, not for a specific target.
- **How much to polish creatives** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): GameMakers (T18440): test fast without over-polishing; a wacky ad can beat a careful one. | Existing file rule: finalise thumbnails and icons before launch (treat as the older, more conservative position).
- **How many creatives to test** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): GameMakers (N7880, T18433): produce 20-50 concepts per winner. | Deconstructor of Fun (N7981): smaller publishers under $1M/quarter struggle to keep up with top spenders' creative volume, so tiny teams should aim lower. | janisjanis (T18625): a few sequential campaigns, stopping weak ones, was enough for a small game.
- **Audience versus messaging segmentation** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing file rule: use broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players. | GameMakers (T18435): segment by motivation persona so each creative speaks to one motivation. | janisjanis (T18709): all-player audience for the first campaign. These are compatible: persona segmentation happens in creative, while audience stays broad.
- **Campaign goal naming (Roblox)** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing file rule: set the ad goal to Maximize Plays. | janisjanis (T18709, Aug 2026): goal named 'Players'. Likely a UI label change; verify in Ads Manager before relying on either name.
- **Stop after seeding versus always-on channels** (ads-and-sponsored-experiences): Existing file rule: use ads to feed the algorithm, then stop. | Deconstructor of Fun (T18667): keep influencer marketing as a permanent pillar. These address different channels, so both can hold.
- **AI in player-facing creation** (ai-and-tools): Roblox official: promotes AI tools (Q420, N1300) | RobloxOfficial speaker: players said they did not want AI use (Q647, T3242)
- **Use of mobile Build for testing and monetization** (ai-and-tools): Existing knowledge rule: use mobile Build for reviews and small edits only. | Roblox Newsroom (T17682, 2026-09, official): mobile Build can be used to test ideas quickly, analyze and add monetization, with in-app ad launches planned. | Resolution: prefer the newer Sept 2026 official guidance; the older rule is outdated for idea testing and monetization, though heavier editing still belongs in Stu…
- **Ads vs fixing benchmarks first** (analytics-metrics): CallMeDogey: fix benchmarks below 75th first, ahead of advertising. | MoseyTube: run ads early, then from day 5 stop them if retention is poor. | Tizzy: his ad-driven plays (~45K) dwarfed home-recommendation plays (~950) in one case (self-reported).
- **Ad CTR target** (analytics-metrics): Yann (N2782): 2.5% or better, with niche simulators around 2.5% and towers up to 8%. | janisjanis (N2675): target 3%+ (first ad reached 2.26%).
- **Playtime threshold** (analytics-metrics): VuukStudios: 10–15 min ideal, 12+ target, 10+ during ads, 20+ on algorithm, 25+ excellent (N5019, T13702, N5375) | PineappleDev: ~13 min benchmark, 15+ min for good Premium payouts (N4596) | Matt The Method: ~22 min as key promotion signal (N5036)
- **Ad attribution method** (analytics-metrics): 2020 DevForum: 1-day view, 28-day click-through (N4209) | 2026 official: CTR, CPC, conversion and CPP from attributed purchases (T14837); attribution window not restated
- **How to read engagement drops during a paid campaign** (analytics-metrics): LovJak (T16546, Q1596): drops are normal because paid players are cold; judge over a longer window and they should improve. | RoyalDev (T16616): check day-one retention and session length before spending on ads; poor numbers mean the game loop must be fixed first.
- **Like-ratio threshold for action** (analytics-metrics): Poke (T16362): a 29% like rate early was a stop-and-fix signal. | Mulch (N6397): ~70–71% mid-campaign was read as acceptable despite early downvote bombing when the game was broken.
- **Cohort evaluation window** (analytics-metrics): GameMakers (2022-08, N7892, Q1878) reviews install cohorts roughly monthly against the previous cohort. | Deconstructor of Fun (2026-06, T18651, T18678) judges creator campaigns on day-365 or lifetime installs, not short windows.
- **Cash out vs reinvest/sell** (business-and-payouts): SmartyRBX says reinvest most profit, but also tells people to convert Robux to cash through DevEx. | CallMeDogey cashes out what he needs and keeps the rest for ads. | DevForum 2022 author says avoid DevEx altogether. | Official docs just describe DevEx; the 18+ rate change makes it somewhat better than the 2022 advice assumed.
- **Source of UGC income** (business-and-payouts): Britt Drake (Q1575) says many UGC promoters earn mainly from courses, not from UGC items. | Creators who sell items directly (#7Phantom, ThePurpleGuy) report marketplace earnings and costs.
- **Size of the creator economy** (business-and-payouts): NetInfluencer (N7501, indie, low-advice): global creator earnings more than $1.5B in 2025. | Roblox DevForum and PocketGamer.biz (N7521, N7505): DevEx payouts about $1.7B in the trailing 12 months. | These measure different things (global creator earnings vs DevEx payouts) and are not reconciled. Do not combine them.
- **Ads and recommendations** (common-mistakes): Tizzy: not updating is why ad spend doesn't get you on the algorithm. | Roblox Creator Docs (2026): ad-acquired users are excluded from RFY signals, so an RFY drop after ads is not a penalty.
- **When to check ad performance versus when to spend: janisjanis01 (T6503) says check ad performance early and replace creative at about 1.9% CTR; janisjanis01 (T6344, T6430) and Duckable (T6550) say stabilise before buying ads. Reconcile: run a small creative check early, but do not scale spend before the build is stable.** (common-mistakes): janisjanis01 (T6503): check ad performance early. | janisjanis01 (T6344, T6430) and Duckable (T6550): stabilise the game before buying ads.
- **Where to promote the game** (community-and-discord): SmartyRBX: promote in Discord promo channels only if allowed (T291), but avoid game-dev servers for player traffic (T292). | AlvinBlox: share with dev communities for feedback (T1803). | BruceDevs: advertise to interested players on Discord before paying for sponsors (T2415).
- **Single social platform vs multi-platform audience** (community-and-discord): Existing knowledge rule: pick one social platform and do it well. | studio rook. (2026-08): build on Instagram and TikTok before release; Instagram drove millions of views during development.
- **Where to keep the community: off-platform Discord vs. on Roblox** (community-and-discord): Roblox [OFFICIAL] (Q1771, 2025-07): 'Let's just keep everyone on Roblox and we're going to get very good at that.' | Existing file rules (creator not named in the file): 'Funnel all players and followers into one Discord' and 'Brand the group and link off-platform socials'.
- **Do ads help or hurt?** (discovery-algorithm): Roblox Creators: ads do not hurt organic discovery and can help cold start. | Erthlord (unverified): bad first ad round can hurt a place. | Erthlord, LovJak, Raul: ads alone did not trigger pickup. | MoseyTube, janisjanis01, Rapstrike GFX: ads got the game into the algorithm.
- **Whether ads or monetisation hurt discovery** (discovery-algorithm): Existing knowledge rule: ads do not hurt organic discovery; use them as a data seed. | Platform Aeronaut (T17498, indie, self-reported): games built around short-term monetisation get fewer impressions, especially with US under-13 audiences. Official sources are silent on ads; treat as unresolved and test.
- **When to stop ads after pickup** (discovery-algorithm): janisjanis01 (2026-08): stop ads once player count is above 50 after the ad ends, and Home recommendations appear. | Existing file (undated): give a new game 7-14 days with ads, then decide; stop once Home recommendations sustain the game.
- **Creator videos vs paid ads** (influencer-and-creator-marketing): janisjanis01: creator videos were a better source than paid Roblox ads. | Deconstructor of Fun: influencers should complement paid UA, not compete with it. | VuukStudios: ads on major updates can revive a game.
- **Building socials early** (influencer-and-creator-marketing): bubDEV: pre-release hype on social platforms builds a loyal base. | VuukStudios: don't build Discord or TikTok before the game is good.
- **When to add monetisation at launch** (monetisation-gamepasses): Raul (T13655): add gamepasses before running ads; monetisation must be ready at launch. | VuukStudios (T13771): keep monetisation bare-bones at release, focus on retention, lock in the full plan after home recommendations. | VuukStudios (T13428): build proper monetisation from the start because the algorithm rewards it.
- **Release gate versus ship-fast cadence** (playtesting-and-feedback): Indie DevForum: do not release until a full pass has no critical issues, since fixes can introduce bugs (T11621). | VuukStudios and Quez: ship fixes and updates while ads run, between ad days (T13823, T12670).
- **Retention loss handling (ads/traffic dips)** (retention-d1-d7): RoyalDev: weak retention from ads reflects game quality. | ItsZyonic: don't judge by CCU dropping when ads stop. | Yann: drop may come from similar-game players leaving.
- **Paying/commissioning influencers** (social-features-and-virality): RobloxOfficial 2018 and AlvinBlox: promo codes, payment in items, commissioning small creators | Roblox (2025, Ninja Challenge): skip paying YouTubers/TikTokers early; a fun game attracts creators organically | SmartyRBX: needs a very good game and lots of value
- **Viral short-form as the main route** (social-features-and-virality): MonzterDEV (T3625, Q721): viral TikTok/Shorts content is the main route to new users and affiliate earnings. Low-advice. | DitchyDevelopments (T3831, T3833): build an interested audience through communities and devlogs; they credit devlog Shorts from day one for their traffic.
- **How much TikTok drives player counts** (social-features-and-virality): rasdev (T6855, 2026-10): TikTok is the biggest external traffic source and 100k-500k views can drive large player counts. Preferred as the more recent view. | janisjanis (T6396, 2026-01): short-form TikTok alone gave only a slight, temporary CCU increase; bumps fade within a day. Based on early-account experience.
- **Whether to pay TikTok creators early** (social-features-and-virality): 1Coal paid about $400 for a TikTok creator video that reached 4M views (T12074). | The existing file rule says to skip early paid influencers, but it applies to Twitter. It does not address TikTok paid creators.
- **Which platform to prioritize for item promotion** (social-features-and-virality): Erthlord's January 2026 reels favored TikTok (17,000+ views vs 6,000 YouTube) (N5078). | Erthlord's July 2026 data favors YouTube (108,000 vs under 5,000 TikTok) for item promos (T13624). The more recent data should be preferred.
- **Which creators to approach first** (social-features-and-virality): Emancyphur (T15840): early coverage by popular Roblox YouTubers drove large player growth without paid advertising. | Existing rule 'Approach smaller creators first with a playable build' favors smaller creators first. | Reconciliation: large channels can drive reach, while small creators give early feedback. Sources do not say which is better for a given budget.
- **Which short-form platform to prioritize** (social-features-and-virality): #7Phantom (2025-07): TikTok grew a 40K group, with a 3 million view breakout after about 5 months of near-daily posting. | studio rook. (2026-08): TikTok averaged about 2 views per video during development, while Instagram pushed videos to millions. | Existing rule 'Post short-form on TikTok/Twitter consistently' does not address Instagram. Both results may be true for different games and timing;…
- **Which platform to prioritize for organic promotion** (social-features-and-virality): SmartyRBX (2023-02, T18473, T18477, T18478): focuses on Twitter engagement, Spaces and devlogs. | janisjanis01 (2026-08, T18712): favors TikTok and YouTube Shorts for zero-budget promotion. Newer advice; prefer it for short-video reach, while Twitter tactics may be dated.
- **Hiring** (studio-business-funding-and-publishing): Roblox DevForum (2017): avoid paid builders and scripters. | TheDevKing: funds can go to art, hiring and ads. | VuukStudios and janisjanis01: partner or team up as the game grows.
- **Swap poor thumbnails early versus keep several active** (thumbnails-icons): Existing knowledge file: early launch window, swap poor-CTR thumbnails. | Roblox Creator Docs (T3862, Q778): keep multiple thumbnails active instead of picking a winner early; removing them too soon loses impressions from changing user trends. | Reconciliation: swap clearly weak ad creatives in campaigns, but keep the Home personalization set at 2–5 active.
- **Sponsored ads: icon versus 16:9 thumbnail** (thumbnails-icons): BubuTheDev (T16146): the icon drives clicks in the sponsored tab, so invest in it first. | Existing knowledge file: prefer 16:9 thumbnails over icons for sponsored ads. Test both before committing.
- **AI-generated icons and vector art** (thumbnails-icons): Existing knowledge file rule: do not use raw in-game screenshots or AI-generated art as icons. | RoyalDev (T17897, Q1836, 2026-10, self-reported, promotional): AI tools make game icons and vector art much easier, and icon reframing can be done with AI. | Resolution: keep the existing caution until icon CTR tests with AI-assisted art show a result; RoyalDev's advice is newer but weaker evidence.
- **Volume vs quality** (trailers-and-video-marketing): VuukStudios: post short-form daily and long-form weekly. | janisjanis01 (June 2025): higher-effort, fewer videos outperformed volume, though earlier in May 2025 and elsewhere he stressed consistency. | #7Phantom: invest in good ads but also post 2-3x/week minimum.
- **Trailer CTA/title card vs platform rules** (trailers-and-video-marketing): rasdev recommends a title card with call to action and 60-90s trailer. | Roblox DevForum: on-platform videos must not have promo text, voiceover or cinematic styling, so rasdev's structure applies only to external trailers.
- **How early to ramp promo around launch** (trailers-and-video-marketing): Cumin (T17806): start trailers earlier than other promo, but go to full promo speed only near release. | Existing knowledge-file rule 'Start posting short-form promo early, even when the game is rough': begin short-form posting early regardless of polish. Not a direct conflict. Cumin refines the timing of paid or full-tempo promo, not whether to post early.
- **Continue or abandon a weak post-launch game** (updates-live-ops): VuukStudios (T13436, T13632): stop updating and start a new project if the game gets no home recommendations or fails to hook after about 2 weeks of daily updates. | Q1412 (VuukStudios): a game is only finished when it's dead; release does not mean finished. | Matt The Method (T13176): earnings collapsed once updates and promotion stopped; keep updating and promoting to sustain revenue.
